## 2026-11-05 — Cycle 55 (improve): P2 item 16/13 — templates as pure builder functions COMPLETED

- Implemented P2 item 16/13: `hydra/starter-templates.js` is now a pure builder module — no DOM, no storage, no `window` at load time, no shared mutable state between builds.
- **Headless load:** the bare `window.HydraStarterTemplates = {...}` footer is replaced with the hydra-flow.js pattern — a `typeof window !== 'undefined'` guard plus `module.exports` — so the file loads in plain node via `require()` with no `window` global at all. The core is a dependency, not an assumed global: `createProject(templateId, projectName, idFactory, core)` resolves it as the explicit 4th argument first, then `globalThis.HydraUniversal` (in a browser that is `window`; in a node vm sandbox it is the context global), and throws a plain-language error naming HydraUniversal when no core is available instead of a TypeError on an undefined global.
- **Optional id factory:** the factory is passed through to the core instead of being called eagerly — without one the core mints the project id itself (cycle 53 behavior), so a caller that does not care about ids can omit it entirely. Old 3-argument calls (hub, editor, existing tests) are unchanged in behavior.
- **Scene builders exposed:** `buildScene(templateId)` and `buildScenes(templateId)` join the exported API, so headless callers can build one scene or a template's whole scene list (flagship 8, fullgame 4) without building a project.
- **Purity, test-enforced:** new `tests/templates-pure.test.js` (58 checks) proves the module loads via plain `require()` with no window, core injection works with no global HydraUniversal present, global resolution works when one is set, a missing core throws the clear error, the id factory and name are optional, the scene builders are fresh per call, unknown ids fall back to a blank scene/project, the raw scene builders are byte-identical across calls, full projects match modulo the core's random `cmp_*` component ids and wall-clock timestamps, and — for every one of the 18 templates — two builds share no object references (identity walk over the whole project tree) and mutating the first build leaves the second byte-identical.
- **Test-authoring fixes (mine, not engine bugs):** (1) my first fullgame scene-count check said 2 — fullGameScenes() actually returns 4 (Main Menu, Level 1, Level 2, You Win), fixed the expectation and pinned the names; (2) my first determinism check compared full projects byte-for-byte, but the core normalizer mints fresh random `cmp_*` component ids on every call, so two normalized projects can never be byte-identical — the check now compares the raw builders byte-for-byte and the normalized projects after stripping the core-minted ids and timestamps (the core's randomness, not the templates', documented in the test).
- **Firewall check updated:** `tests/core.test.js` asserted the literal `window.HydraUniversal.createProject({` in starter-templates.js — the new resolution form is `U.createProject({` off `globalThis.HydraUniversal`, so the check now asserts that form and additionally that no `window.HydraUniversal` reference survives in the file (strictly stronger, not weaker).
- Docs: ROADMAP P2 items 16 and 13 checked off; CHANGELOG entry added.
- Next cycle: remaining P2 items are the editor as a DOM-only module (14), and the shared-engine extraction (15/12, highest risk, do last). Also open: the recorded v4 gap, a Screen Flash block.

## 2026-11-05 — Cycle 54 (improve): P2 item 14/11 — block catalog aligned with the v4 field-control schema COMPLETED

- Implemented P2 item 14/11: the field-control schema is now explicit and machine-checked instead of implicit in `script.js`'s property-panel switch.
- **Schema (`hydra/block-catalog.js`):** `HydraBlockCatalog.FIELD_CONTROLS` names the canonical ten controls, each with the only extra keys a field with that control may carry: the v4 six (text/textarea/expr → placeholder, textarea also rows; object → includeAll; select → options; color → none) plus the v3 extensions (number → min/max/step; checkbox/action/scene → none). `validateCatalog(list?)` is pure and never throws (explicit null reports as a caller error rather than silently defaulting to the catalog and reading valid; non-array/garbage input returns findings); it enforces block identity (category/label/color/cellLabel/note present, kind in condition/action/meta, defaults.type === runtimeType, unique blockType), field shape (key/label/control present, control in the vocabulary, no unexpected extra keys), select options as non-empty [value, label] pairs, number min/max/step with min ≤ max, expr/text/textarea placeholder present, `reversed` in the canonical `[[false,'Off'],[true,'On']]` select shape, and every field key present in defaults. `findBlock(blockType)` is the safe lookup (null for unknown/non-string). The header comment documents the schema and the by-design preset realities the validator deliberately does not flag: duplicate runtimeType (presets share their base type — key presets are keyPressed/keyJustPressed with inputCondition pinning the mode, mouse presets are all mouseClick, and so on), defaults keys with no field (preset-pinned values), and blocks with no fields at all (fixed presets).
- **The one real drift, fixed:** the Music Playing block declared an Invert (`reversed`) field but its defaults were `{ type: 'musicPlaying' }` with no `reversed` key — a freshly placed block's Invert read fell back to `''` instead of a real boolean. Defaults are now `{ type: 'musicPlaying', reversed: false }`. The audit confirmed this was the only field-not-in-defaults case in the catalog (the other 296 scan hits were all the by-design preset pattern above).
- **v4 alignment (v4-reference read-only, never edited):** all 38 v4 blocks accounted for — in the catalog, dispatched by a runtime, via an explicit rename map in the test (keyDown/keyReleased → keyPressed with inputCondition, mouseDown/mousePressed → mouseClick with mouseCondition, overlap → overlapTouch, timer → onTimer, moveToward → moveTowardObject — v3 split it, also moveTowardPosition — applyImpulse → applyForce, variableCheck → getVariable, cameraShake → screenShake, createParticles → emitBurst), or the one recorded gap: v4's **screenFlash has no v3 equivalent** (no fullscreen-flash action exists), pinned as `KNOWN_GAPS = ['screenFlash']` so adding it updates the list instead of silently dropping a v4 capability. Every v4 control is in FIELD_CONTROLS. Running the validator over the v4 reference itself pinned two v4 facts: 18 v4 blocks predate the tooltip-notes rule, and v4's play-sound block has a genuine authoring bug — `defaults` declares `type` twice (`{ type: 'playSound', ..., type: 'sine' }`, the wave field reuses the discriminator key), so its effective defaults.type is `'sine'`; v3 sidesteps it (Play Sound is asset-based, no wave field).
- Test-authoring fixes (mine, not engine bugs): (1) the first `validateCatalog(null)` check failed because `list || this.blocks` treated an explicit null as "no argument" and returned [] — the method now defaults only on `undefined`, so an explicit null reports as a caller error; (2) my first v4-conformance check assumed the v4 reference conformed, but it does not (the 18 missing notes + the play-sound bug) — the test now pins that exact finding list and validates the repaired copy for the field-control shape instead.
- Tests: new `tests/catalog.test.js` (70 checks: vocabulary, schema extras, shipped-catalog conformance, every control rendered by script.js's makeCatalogFieldInput, findBlock, the music-playing fix, preset realities, hostile-input safety, per-rule validator findings, v4 control coverage + rename map + known gap); 3 new `tests/browser-smoke.js` checks (live validateCatalog() clean, music-playing default, FIELD_CONTROLS live). Full suite ALL green.
- Docs: ROADMAP P2 items 14 and 11 checked off; CHANGELOG entry added.
- Next cycle: remaining P2 items are templates as pure builders (16/13 — largely true already: starter-templates.js builds through the core factories and is tested headlessly in node; needs a verification pass, not a build), the editor as a DOM-only module (14), and the shared-engine extraction (15/12, highest risk, do last). Also open: the recorded v4 gap, a Screen Flash block.

## 2026-11-05 — Cycle 53 (improve): P2 item 13/10 — core factories behind the firewall COMPLETED

- Implemented the P2 core item: `HydraUniversal` is now the ONLY place that builds project/scene/object records from scratch. Three factories in `hydra/hydra-universal.js`, each returning its record through the matching normalizer, so nothing unnormalized can ever enter the store:
  - `createScene(options)` — the exact record `script.js createScene()` used to build by hand (white background, 800×600, empty grids), normalized on return. Callers pass the id (the editor's `nextSceneId`); a missing id is minted from a module counter seeded at `Date.now()`, the same convention `script.js` used for `nextObjectId`.
  - `createObject(kind, options)` — the whole kind-defaults table `addGameObject()` carried inline (sizes, display names, colors, 3D fields, text fields, light records, the `{gravity 0, friction 0.82, bounciness 0.4, maxSpeed 25}` physics default kept in sync with `DEFAULT_PHYSICS` in `script.js`), normalized on return. The requested kind is kept as `archetype`; the stored `kind` follows the editor's mapping (button → text, empty/camera3d → sprite). A requested `'light'` is not resolved here — the editor still maps it to light3d/light2d from the scene's render mode first.
  - `createProject(options)` — blank-project shape (one Scene 1, counters derived from the scenes given, `idFactory` or an explicit id, extra fields like `tutorial`/`playableRuntime`/`fpsConfig` passing through), normalized on return.
- **Construction sites routed (the firewall):** `script.js createScene()` and `addGameObject()` (the object is built at the click point, then centered on it and snapped to the scene grid; the Transform component's stored x/y are synced after the snap, or editing that component later would jump the object back to the pre-snap position — the one subtle behavior the transplant had to preserve), `project.js createNewProject()` (keeps the editor's dark `#1e1e1e` blank-scene background, unlike the factory's white default), `starter-templates.js createProject()` (template flags ride through as extra fields), and the `home.js` fallback blank project used when the templates fail to load. No second construction literal survives in any of those files — `tests/core.test.js` asserts that statically.
- **Test-authoring fixes (mine, not engine bugs):** (1) the first `createObject` version left `hitboxWidth` at the kind default when a caller overrode `width` — the factory now syncs the hitbox to the final size unless the caller overrides the hitbox itself, restoring the old literal's invariant; (2) the first box3d/plane3d checks expected a depth without Hydra3d loaded — the sandbox now loads `hydra-3d.js` so the 3D path (Box3D name, depth, z 0) is tested for real; (3) the smoke object-count wait first waited for the pre-click total, and the known-ids set was built with `new Set` inside `evaluate` (which does not survive Playwright serialization) — now an array, counted before the click.
- **Test sandboxes now mirror the real load order:** `tests/templates.test.js`, `tests/tutorial.test.js`, and the `tplSandbox` in `tests/export-boot.test.js` loaded `starter-templates.js` without the core and called `createProject` — they now load `hydra-universal.js` first, as `index.html`/`home.html` do.
- **P0 items 1–3 checked off (done, never marked):** the v3 deep inspection and the architecture comparison are complete in PROGRESS.md below (the stale "v3 inspection in progress" line is corrected), the working tree and local dev process are documented in `README.md`, and `tests/run.js` exists with item 4's verification behind it. **Item 37 (block catalog expansion) checked off:** its coverage checklist is empty — all 12 batches done. The two P2 sections in ROADMAP.md duplicate each other (items 13–16 vs 10–14); the core item is checked off in both listings.
- **Deferred, not done:** export-time normalization. `buildPlayableHtml` still embeds the project as-is (the exported runtime has no HydraUniversal inlined, so the firewall would have to clone + normalize at build time). It is safe today because every export comes from editor state that was already normalized on load, so this stays a follow-up for a cycle with a real damaged-export case, not a change made on speculation.
- Tests: new `tests/core.test.js` (factory defaults/overrides/hostile-input checks, every template building normalized through the factory, the firewall static checks); 3 new `tests/browser-smoke.js` checks (Add scene creates a normalized factory scene, Create Sprite builds a 60×60 factory object, Transform props match the snapped position). Full suite ALL green.
- Docs: ROADMAP P2 item 13/10, P0 items 1–3, and item 37 checked off; CHANGELOG entry added.
- Next cycle: remaining P2 items are the block-catalog alignment (11/14), the shared-engine extraction (12/15, highest risk, do last), templates as pure builders (13/16 — partly done: templates now build through the core factories), and the editor as a DOM-only module (14).

## 2026-11-05 — Cycle 52 (improve): P3 item 17 — accessibility pass COMPLETED

- Implemented P3 item 17: the editor now works without a mouse.
- **Logic grid keyboard navigation (`hydra/script.js`):** the 3600 cells use roving tabindex (one Tab stop for the whole grid). A delegated keydown handler on `#grid` moves focus with the arrow keys (clamped at the edges), Home/End jump to the ends of a row, Enter/Space activates the focused cell through the same `activateCell` the click handler now calls (place the selected block or select the block already there), Delete/Backspace clears a placed block on the focused cell, and Escape returns focus to the grid container (where the pre-existing document-level handler still scrolls the grid with the arrows). The handler stops propagation so the scroll handler never double-fires. Every cell carries a plain-language `aria-label` ("Row 8, column 8: Game Start block" / "Row 8, column 8: empty cell") via `updateCellA11y`, refreshed inside `applyBlockVisualToCell`/`clearBlockVisualFromCell` and on selection (`selectCell` moves the roving stop to the selected cell); the grid container's own aria-label explains the keys.
- **Block palette keyboard navigation:** palette options are `role="button"` with an aria-label taken from their block label, one Tab stop via roving tabindex, arrows move between visible options, Enter/Space picks the tool, and the search filter moves the Tab stop to the first visible match so a filtered list never strands focus on a hidden option. Clicking an option moves the roving stop to it.
- **Names for everything:** every icon-only button in the editor and hub gained an `aria-label` (scene toolbar, object tools, preview restart/profiler/trace, hub wizard close, notifications, grid/list view toggles, project-card rename/backup/delete in `home.js`, the dynamic localization and input-action delete buttons). Property-panel labels are bound to their controls: `makePropGroup` assigns a unique id and sets `label.htmlFor`, and a new `bindStaticPropGroupLabels` binds the bare labels in the static scene-settings groups at load. The block search, entity search, preview device select, sort select, hub search, and new-object-kind select are named, the wizard's Project Name / Description labels use `for`, and the seven scene-settings controls that had no static name gained `aria-label`s. The diagnostics feed is `aria-live="polite"`.
- **Dialog focus management:** the Export dialog focuses its Close button on open and returns focus to the Export Game button on close; the New Project dialog already focused its name field and now also returns focus to the New button; the block picker closes on Escape and returns focus to the button that opened it (it already focused its search box on open).
- **Responsive + reduced motion (`hydra/style.css`):** below 900px the editor stacks its side panels below the main area (inspector under the stage, properties panel under the grid, block picker in one column) instead of squeezing them beside it, and a `prefers-reduced-motion` rule turns off transitions and animations.
- Test-authoring fixes (mine, not engine bugs): (1) the palette Enter test first failed because the picker flow leaves a cell selected, which hides the palette behind the properties panel so `focus()` on an option is a silent no-op — the test now clicks an empty cell with the eraser active first, as a user would; (2) my first palette test regexes used `case` where the handler uses `if`; (3) the pre-existing F1 hint test pressed Escape and then clicked the picker's Close button — Escape was previously a no-op in the picker, so the click worked, but now Escape closes the picker and the click timed out on a detached modal; the test now asserts Escape closes the picker and reopens it to test the Close button.
- Tests: new `tests/accessibility.test.js` (27 checks: every button and visible control named in both HTML files, grid + palette keyboard wiring, label binding, dialog focus, responsive + reduced-motion CSS); 18 new `tests/browser-smoke.js` checks (palette arrows/Enter, grid arrows/Home/Enter-place/Delete/Escape, dialog focus in/out, narrow-viewport stacking, live region, icon-button names, Escape + Close-button picker closing). Full suite ALL green.
- Docs: user guide gained the Accessibility section; ROADMAP item 17 checked off; CHANGELOG entry added.
- Next cycle: remaining items are the P2 modularization items (extract core, block catalog alignment, templates as pure builders, editor as DOM-only module; the shared-engine extraction stays last as the highest-risk item).

## 2026-11-05 — Cycle 51 (improve): P3 item 18 — block validation diagnostics COMPLETED

- Implemented P3 item 18: dangling object/scene references and invalid expressions in a project's logic grids are now caught statically and reported in the editor's diagnostics dock, instead of only failing silently (or with a runtime warning) at preview time.
- **Validator (`hydra/hydra-universal.js`):** `collectProjectDiagnostics` is now a real static validator, keeping its old empty-block and unnamed-timer checks. Field roles are derived from the declarative block catalog at call time (control `object` → object-name field, `expr` → expression field, `scene` → scene field, merged across duplicate runtimeType entries) plus a `LEGACY_FIELDS` map for the 27 core block types the catalog does not cover (collision, overlapTouch, onEdge, applyForce, teleport, destroyObject, showHide, playAnimation/setAnimation/stopAnimation/animationFinished, setAnimState/animStateIs, setBlend/stopBlend/blendActive, tweenProperty/stopTween/tweenFinished, switchScene, setText, setSize, setVelocity, spawnObject, rotateObject, randomVariable, forceToward). Every block in the Global grid is checked against the union of all scenes' object names (Global logic runs everywhere) and every scene grid against that scene's objects only; scene ids are checked for switchScene/pushScene. Findings carry scope + cell index and are capped at 100 so one broken import cannot flood the panel.
- **Expression checking is syntax-only:** new `HydraExpr.validateSyntax` in `hydra-expr.js` compiles with `new Function` but never runs the code, so a bare variable name is valid even before the variable exists (variables are created at runtime) while `score +`, `(5`, `Math.floor(`, `foo bar`, and `5 + * 3` are all caught. Collections blocks whose valueKind is `text` or `variable` hold plain text in their `value` field, not an expression, so that field is skipped (an item name like "Iron Sword" never trips the checker). Without the catalog or HydraExpr loaded, the validator degrades gracefully: legacy object checks still run, expression checks are skipped, nothing throws.
- **Editor surfacing:** new `runBlockValidation(project)` on HydraUniversal removes the previous validation entries from the live diagnostics feed first (fixing a block clears its warning; runtime diagnostics are untouched), pushes the new findings with source `{kind: 'validation', scope, index}`, and re-renders the dock. `script.js` gained `refreshValidationDiagnostics()` (validates the live `window.scenes` + `window.globalGridData`), called on every grid commit (`commitActiveGrid`), on object delete, and on both scene-delete paths; `project.js` calls it once on project load next to `renderDiagnostics()`. Also hardened `renderDiagnostics()` with a `typeof document` guard so the validator is callable from node test sandboxes.
- Tests: new `tests/validation.test.js` (48 checks: validateSyntax valid/broken cases, dangling object refs incl. the global-union vs scene-only rule, dangling scene refs, legacy + catalog expression checks, the valueKind text/variable exemption, existing empty-block/timer checks, every starter template passing clean, feed refresh semantics incl. runtime entries surviving, the 100 cap, bare-sandbox degradation, damaged project never throwing); 8 new `tests/browser-smoke.js` checks (seeded broken project: dock reports the ghost object, both broken expressions, and the missing scene; the good Global-grid object stays quiet; count >= 4; fixing the blocks through the live editor state clears the dock to "No runtime diagnostics."; zero page errors). Full suite ALL green.
- Docs: user-guide Preview and diagnostics section gained the block-validation paragraph; ROADMAP item 18 checked off; CHANGELOG entry added.
- Next cycle: remaining items are the P2 modularization items (highest risk: the shared engine module, do last) and the accessibility pass (17).

## 2026-11-05 — Cycle 50 (improve): P1 item 16 — resilience COMPLETED

- Implemented P1 item 16: corrupted project data now normalizes or reports, never hard-crashes the editor. The normalizer itself was the hole: `normalizeProject` threw on a string where the scene list belongs (`(project.scenes || []).forEach is not a function`), `normalizeScene` threw on a string object list the same way, and `rebuildProjectEventSheets` dereferenced null scene entries — so one damaged record took the whole hub list down (the old hub `loadProjects` mapped all records in one shot and fell back to `[]` on any throw, and a later save would then overwrite the store).
- **Normalizer hardening (`hydra/hydra-universal.js`):** new `asArray`/`asPlainObject` coercions repair wrong-type collections in place — `scenes`, `objects`, `layers`, `eventSheets` repair to their empty defaults; `logicGrids`/`globalGridData`/`gridData`/`assets`/`collision`/`variables` repair to `{}` with the grid aliases re-pointed at the repaired object either way; `collectAssetUsage`/`removeAssetReferences`/`rebuildProjectEventSheets`/`collectProjectDiagnostics` skip damaged scene entries instead of dereferencing them; `serializeProject` returns a circular project unchanged instead of dying inside the clone, so the save path can report the failure.
- **Loader isolation (hub + editor):** both `loadProjects` paths now parse the store once, back the raw bytes up under a dated `hydraProjects.corrupt.<timestamp>` key when the JSON is invalid or not a list, and repair/drop records one at a time (a dropped record is counted and reported, the good ones stay). The hub shows a visible amber banner above the project list (inline styles, so it renders even with the Tailwind CDN down) and its `saveProjects` refuses to overwrite a damaged store while no backup exists; the editor reports a damaged store once with an alert and opens a fresh project. `initProject()` is wrapped so a bad load can never kill the editor's DOMContentLoaded handler (save/export/import wiring still runs), `saveBtn` wiring tolerates a missing button, and `createNewProject` survives a full store.
- **Top-level element guards (`hydra/script.js`):** `#grid` and `#game-canvas` are looked up at load time and a missing element used to kill the whole script (`canvas.getContext` on null, `grid.appendChild` on null). Both now fall back to a detached element of the same kind via a `fallbackElement` helper (logged to the console), so the rest of the editor keeps working and the affected pane simply renders nothing.
- Test-authoring fix (mine, not an engine bug): the first smoke version seeded the corrupt store with `addInitScript`, which re-clobbered the store on every later navigation in that context and made the (b)/(c) checks fail against a store that was corrupt again, not seeded — reseeded with a plain evaluate + reload instead.
- Tests: new `tests/resilience.test.js` (37 checks: normalizer coercions, scanner tolerance, circular serialize, the save-fidelity no-regression checks, static wiring checks for the guards and both loaders); 10 new `tests/browser-smoke.js` checks (corrupt store banner + backup wording + hub still renders + backup key exists, good project survives a damaged sibling, repairable record is repaired and listed, unrepairable record reported in the banner, editor opens a project with a damaged scenes field with the grid still rendering 3600 cells, zero page errors). Full suite ALL green.
- Docs: user guide gained the "Damaged saved data" paragraph; ROADMAP item 16 checked off; CHANGELOG entry added.
- Next cycle: remaining items are the P2 modularization items (highest risk: the shared engine module, do last), the accessibility pass (17), and block validation diagnostics (18).

## 2026-11-05 — Cycle 49 (improve): P3 item 15 — editor project JSON import/export + templates COMPLETED

- Implemented P3 item 15 (the hub already had these; the editor had neither): the editor header gained **New**, **Import JSON**, and **Export JSON** buttons. Export JSON saves the current grid first, then downloads the serialized project as a `.hydra` file via the existing `downloadBlob`. Import JSON reads a `.hydra` file with the same record hygiene as the hub import (id sanitized, name fallback, scenes/assets defaults, `normalizeProject`); a colliding id gets a fresh id instead of overwriting, and a broken file alerts and leaves the current project untouched. Both import and New open the result by reloading through the normal load path (assets, scenes, grids, inspector) instead of partially re-rendering live stores. New saves the current project first, then opens a dialog with a name field and the full template list from `HydraStarterTemplates` (per-template description, Escape/overlay/X/Cancel close) and creates the project with `createProject`, with a blank fallback if the templates script fails to load. `starter-templates.js` is now loaded in `index.html` before `project.js`.

Full suite ALL green.

## 2026-11-05 — Cycle 46 (improve): P3 item 24 — template logic audit COMPLETED

- Audited every starter template against the real `HydraFlow` edges (a throwaway analyzer in the workflow workdir: per-condition propagate orders + dead-action/dangling-ref scan over all 18 template ids). The first scan found zero structurally dead actions — but the propagate dumps showed the subtler adjacency-accident class, where an action fires from a condition it was never meant for, in the wrong order relative to its siblings.
- **Bugs found and fixed (all in `hydra/starter-templates.js`):**
  - Platformer Quest coin respawn: the collision's propagate order was score, random coinY, setPosition (old coinX + new coinY), random coinX — X lagged one collect behind and was undefined on the first collect. Now one row: collision 901 → score 902 → roll coinX 903 → roll coinY 904 → move coin 905. (Puzzle Platformer is a clone of this scene, so it inherits the fix.)
  - Crystal Key Adventure Level 1: jumping also added score, destroyed the coin, and played its sound (coin row sat below the jump and-block); touching the enemy also set hasKey, destroyed the key, and showed "Key collected" (key row sat above the enemy triggerOnce); collecting the key drifted the player right forever (the hasKey check sat below the ArrowRight force). Door and-block also refreshed the score text. Rewritten one-event-per-row with blank guard rows; jump and door keep the documented and-pattern (Space above the and block, door check above its and block).
  - Crystal Key Adventure Level 2: jumping also applied the ArrowLeft force; touching the enemy also collected the coin, added the gem score, and switched to the win screen (gem row sat below the enemy triggerOnce). Same one-event-per-row rewrite.
  - Normalizations (no behavior change beyond removing duplicate firings): Platformer Quest score text moved onto the clamp row, enemy chase got its own Every Frame row, enemy-hit and goal events became single rows; Top-Down Arena score text moved onto the clamp row, faceMovement joined the chase row, goal and enemy-hit respawns moved onto their collision's row.
  - Audited clean, unchanged: webapp, physics, collector, shooter, 3D Scene, FPS Prototype, Neon Reactor FPS, and all variant clones (towerdefense/roguelike/survival = topdown, racer = physics, rpg = collector). Flagship was not rewritten: its few off-row actions are covered by the end-to-end playthrough in `tests/flagship.test.js` (menu to win, zero diagnostics).
- **Regression test:** new `tests/templates.test.js` — for every template scene: all block types known (legacy lists + catalog kinds), no dead actions, every action reachable from a condition on its own row (flagship exempted, per above) with the documented exception for the action directly below an and-block (the platformer jump velocity), no action reading a variable a later action in the same chain writes, no dangling object names or scene ids; plus golden propagate orders for the platformer coin chain, the fullgame L1 jump/enemy-hit/key-check/door events, and the fullgame L2 jump/enemy-hit/gem events.

## 2026-11-05 — Cycle 43 (improve): P4 item 36 phase C — flow tracer COMPLETED

- Implemented the pending flow tracer (item 36i), closing out P4: **the flow tracer** (Unity/Godot-style live logic debugging, beginner edition). New shared pure module `hydra/hydra-trace.js` (HydraTrace, `factorySource` per the hydra-*.js pattern, inlined into exports via `buildPlayableHtml`, hard load guard in player-runtime.js): `createTracer` (default 200-event cap, bad caps repair), `enable`/`disable`/`toggle`/`isEnabled` (enabling clears so the feed starts fresh; disabling keeps the last trace readable), `record` (no-op while disabled or on unusable scope/index/unknown kind; oldest events drop first past the cap; updates the last-fired map), `recent` (copies, newest window), `clear` (empties events + marks, keeps enabled + cap), `lastFiredFrame`/`firedRecently` (recency window for the grid highlight), `formatEvent` (one plain line per event). Nothing ever throws; hostile input degrades to safe defaults.
- **Editor:** the Preview tab gained a **Trace** toolbar button next to Profiler (active state syncs every frame in `drawGame`), a **Trace panel** overlaid bottom-left on the preview canvas (header + Clear button + feed, built with textContent so project data cannot inject markup), and a cyan `traced` ring on grid cells that fired in the last 30 frames (applied in `processScriptGrid` for the active scope). Recording hooks into the existing flow: the triggering condition records first, then `HydraFlow.propagate`'s `onVisit` records every visited action / condition-pass / condition-stop, in both the preview (`processDeterministicConditions`) and the export (`runCondition`) with identical event order and frame numbers (preview uses `previewState.frameId`, export uses `state.frameId`). The tracer is per-run session state: fresh and disabled on every preview run / exported-game start, never saved into project JSON, surviving scene switches.
- **Exports:** tracing is off by default (a pure no-op); the generated boot script wires `?hydraTrace=1` to enable tracing and expose the ring as `window.__hydraTrace` for devtools inspection (guarded in try/catch so a debug flag can never break the boot).
- Test-authoring notes (mine, not engine bugs): (1) the new hard guard initially broke every earlier export-boot phase, as in past cycles — all module-load sequences in export-boot/fps-export/flagship were bulk-updated for hydra-trace.js (guard-filter lines for other modules deliberately untouched); (2) no other test-authoring fixes were needed — the onVisit hook from the flow module worked as documented.
- Tests: new `tests/trace.test.js` (36 checks: module surface, factorySource, lifecycle incl. idempotence + missing-tracer quiet, recording incl. disabled no-op + kind validation + cap trimming + frame repair, recent copies, clear semantics incl. re-enable clearing marks and disable keeping events, last-fired + recency incl. window edges, formatEvent incl. hostile input, hostile tracer shapes); `tests/export-boot.test.js` Phase AV (15 checks: export inlining, `?hydraTrace=1` boot wiring, missing-module guard, disabled-by-default no-op with the game still running, enable records trigger-then-action in order + level re-triggers every frame + advancing frame numbers + zero diagnostics, disable freezes the ring, re-enable clears + resumes, clear keeps enabled); 10 new `tests/browser-smoke.js` checks (module loaded, Always block placement, Trace toolbar button, panel hidden by default, button lights up, panel shows, feed lists `trigger always`, firing block carries the `traced` highlight, button dims + panel hides on second click). Full suite ALL green.
- Docs: user-guide gained the "Flow tracer" section (the toolbar button, the panel, the grid ring, the 200-event cap, the `?hydraTrace=1` export flag, the honest limits) plus a pointer from "Preview and diagnostics"; ROADMAP item 36i checked off (P4 now COMPLETE) and the Advanced-batch-6 tail note updated; CHANGELOG entry added.

Identical semantics in the editor preview and exported games: per-run current language starting at the project default, surviving scene switches (loadScene/launchPreview apply bound labels but never reset the language), resetting on the next run; unbound texts keep their authored text and keys with no translation anywhere keep the old value.

Nothing ever throws; hostile input degrades to safe defaults.
- **Editor:** new Inspector **Skeleton** section for sprite-drawn objects (same gate as Blend Spaces) — Hide sprite checkbox, a live mini rig preview of the rest pose (fitted to the canvas), per-bone cards (Name, Parent select, Length, Angle, Thickness, color picker, image-asset select), per-animation cards (Name, Speed fps, Mode select, per-track Bone select + comma-separated Angles text, + Add track), + Add bone / + Add animation. Bone renames repoint children's parents, the bone's tracks, and Set Bone Angle blocks aimed at this object; animation renames repoint the three animation blocks; deleting a bone reparents its children to the deleted bone's parent and drops its tracks.
- Four new blocks (block-catalog.js, category **Skeleton**, auto-hydrated into the palette with a Skeleton group + catalog-driven property panels): **Play Skeleton Animation** (action — Object + Animation), **Stop Skeleton Animation** (action — Object + Animation, blank stops the active one), **Set Bone Angle** (action — Object + Bone + Angle expression), and the invertible **Skeleton Playing** (condition — 'level' trigger default in hydra-flow.js, evaluates while paused like a variable read). Identical semantics in the editor preview and exported games: bones draw inside the sprite transform in both draw paths (rotation, flip, scale, camera, and zoom all apply to the rig), Hide sprite skips the sprite rect but still paints bones, and the clock advances inside the unpaused update so poses freeze while paused.
- Test-authoring fixes (mine, not engine bugs): (1) my bulk module-load script appended a bare `'hydra-skeleton.js',` line after single-line array statements in export-boot/flagship (a syntax error in one case, a silent no-load in the other) — repaired by in-line insertion right after `'hydra-joints.js',` in every array, so load order (skeleton before player-runtime) holds; (2) my export guard regex carried a doubled backslash (`/hydra-skeleton\\.js/`) and never matched — fixed to the single-backslash form; (3) my frame-clock export check pumped 8 frames but the play lands on frame 1, so only 7 post-play updates ran (116ms, short of the 125ms step) — now pumps 9.
- **Real bug caught by the smoke test (engine bug, fixed): the Inspector Skeleton section's edits vanished.** `normalizeSkeleton` first replaced `obj.skeleton` with a fresh object on every call, so the section's held `skel` reference went stale the moment the mini rig preview re-normalized mid-build — the + Add bone handler pushed onto an orphaned array (proven by handler instrumentation: afterPush 1, obj.skeleton.bones 0) and every section edit was lost on refresh. Fixed by rewriting `normalizeSkeleton` to repair in place (fields fixed on the existing objects, corrupt entries spliced out), so skeleton identity survives any number of normalizes — also kills per-frame garbage in the draw paths, which normalize every frame. Regression test added (held references survive re-normalization).

## 2026-11-04 — Cycle 40 (improve): Advanced batch 4 — physics joints COMPLETED

- Implemented the fourth Advanced batch: **physics joints** (Box2D joints, beginner edition). New shared pure module `hydra/hydra-joints.js` (HydraJoints, `factorySource` per the hydra-*.js pattern, inlined into exports via `buildPlayableHtml`, hard load guard in player-runtime.js): `normalizeJoints` (blank/self joints dropped, bad types repair to distance, bad lengths become null for capture-on-solve, bad stiffness repairs to 0.05, exact unordered pair + type duplicates collapse), `objectCenter`/`distanceBetween` (pos + half size), `findByName` (first live match), `jointConnected` (either direction; blank B means any joint involving A), `removeJoint` (blank B removes all involving A), `solveOneJoint`/`solveJoints` (two passes per frame; distance rods hold exact length, ropes constrain only past max length, springs are a damped velocity pull toward rest length capped at 30 px/frame so huge stretches cannot explode; static objects are anchors that never move, two moving objects split corrections half and half, null lengths capture the current distance on first solve, missing endpoints do nothing). Nothing ever throws; hostile input degrades to safe defaults.
- **Editor:** new Inspector **Joints** section for 2D physics objects (skips 3D kinds, lights, tilemaps) — lists the scene joints involving the object with Delete per row, plus an add form (Other object select, Type select, Length text blank = current distance, Stiffness number) that re-adds the same pair + type as a replace, so a rod and a spring on one pair coexist. Joints live on the scene, so the section lists joints where the object is either end. A real bug caught by the smoke test: a fresh sprite starts with a blank name, and a joint written with a blank end was silently dropped by normalization on the next render — the section now says plainly that the object needs a name first instead of showing a form that eats the joint. The prompt is a plain visible element, not a `makeNote` help note: property help notes are hidden by default (`SHOW_PROPERTY_HELP = false`), so the first version of the prompt was invisible to users and to `innerText` checks alike.
- Three new blocks (block-catalog.js, category **Physics**, auto-hydrated into the palette with a Physics group + catalog-driven property panels): **Create Joint** (action — Object A + Object B + Type + Length + Stiffness; bad type/length/stiffness/missing endpoint warns + holds, blank stays quiet, blank length captures the current distance), **Remove Joint** (action — Object A + Object B, blank B removes all involving A, quiet by design), and the invertible **Joint Connected** (condition — 'level' trigger default in hydra-flow.js, evaluates while paused like a variable read). Identical semantics in the editor preview and exported games: the solver runs after collision resolution inside the unpaused update in both runtimes, so joints freeze while paused like the rest of the world sim.
- Test-authoring fixes (mine, not engine bugs): (1) my first createJoint implementation removed every joint on the pair before pushing — now it filters only the same pair + type, so other types survive (caught by the replace unit check); (2) the spring double-applied across the solver's two passes (velocity hit 60 on a huge stretch, not the 30 cap) — springs now solve on the first pass only; (3) my chain test expected one call to settle two rods, but Gauss-Seidel-style relaxation converges over frames — the test now runs 12 frames like a real game; (4) my export guard check carried a doubled backslash in the regex (`/hydra-joints\\.js/`) and never matched — fixed to the single-backslash form the other guard checks use.
- Tests: new `tests/joints.test.js` (42 checks: module surface, factorySource, centers, normalization incl. repairs/drops/dedupe, connection queries, removal, rod/rope/spring solving incl. static anchors, weld-at-0, slack, spring settle + explosion cap, length capture, chain convergence, hostile safety, all action dispatches incl. warns + holds + blank quiet + replace semantics, condition pass/reversed/blank/hostile); `tests/export-boot.test.js` Phase AS (17 checks: export inlining, missing-module guard, pre-placed rope holds a falling ball at max length, createJoint through real grid wiring + jointConnected fires + reversed, corrupt type/length/missing-endpoint warns + holds + blank quiet, removeJoint through wiring + condition silent after, jointConnected fires while paused, spring settles in the exported runtime); all module-load sequences in export-boot/fps-export/flagship updated for the new hard guard; `tests/flow.test.js` (jointConnected → level); 9 new `tests/browser-smoke.js` checks (module loaded, all 3 palette blocks + Physics category, Create Joint placement + panel fields, Joint Connected placement + panel fields, Inspector Joints section incl. the unnamed-sprite name prompt, + Add joint writes a rope joint with a captured length). Full suite ALL green.

## 2026-11-04 — Cycle 39 (improve): Advanced batch 3 — 2D lighting/shadows COMPLETED

- Implemented the third Advanced batch: **2D lighting and shadows** (Godot PointLight2D / Unity 2D lights, beginner edition). New shared pure module `hydra/hydra-light2d.js` (HydraLight2d, `factorySource` per the hydra-*.js pattern, inlined into exports via `buildPlayableHtml`, hard load guard in player-runtime.js): `normalizeLight2d` (enabled/radius/color/intensity repair, castShadow default), `normalizeSceneDarkness` (darkness clamped 0-1, tint repair), `collectLights2d` (world-space centers, enabled-skip, value repair), `occluderBoxes`, `shadowPolygon` (silhouette corners found by cutting at the largest angular gap between sorted corner angles — this also handles the atan2 wraparound when the box sits left of the light; light-inside-box and degenerate inputs return null), `projectLights` (world-to-screen with camera + zoom, shadow polys attached per light), `hexToRgba`, `drawDarkness` (offscreen canvas: darkness fill, destination-out radial punch per light, soft color tint, per-light shadow re-darkening so one light's shadow never eats another's pool, then stamped over the world; darkness 0 skips entirely). Nothing ever throws; hostile input degrades to safe defaults.
- **Editor:** the Create palette's Light button is now context-aware (`data-create-kind="light"`, resolved in `addGameObject` by the current scene's renderMode — 2D light in 2D scenes, 3D light in 3D scenes; the Add Entity dropdown offers both kinds explicitly). New `light2d` object kind: invisible and non-physical (collision disabled in both normalizers, skipped by the sprite branch, Menus & HUD, and 2D Collision sections, never drawn as a 2D sprite in preview or exports), shown in the scene editor as a bulb glyph tinted by the light color with a "2D Light" label (mirroring the light3d glyph, own CSS). New Inspector **Lighting** section for every 2D kind — Emit light checkbox, Light radius, Light color, Light intensity, Cast shadow checkbox, plus the darkness note. Scene Settings gained a Darkness slider (0-100) + Darkness tint for 2D scenes (the wrap hides for 3D scenes, synced on render-mode switch and scene switch).
- Three new blocks (block-catalog.js, category **Lighting**, auto-hydrated into the palette with a Lighting group + catalog-driven property panels): **Set Darkness** (action — darkness expression, clamped 0-1, bad values warn + hold), **Set Light** (action — Object + Radius + Color + On/Off; bad radius/color/mode warn + hold, blank stays quiet), and the invertible **Darkness Level** (condition — darkness vs value with <=/>=/==/!=/</> operators; 'level' trigger default in hydra-flow.js, evaluates while paused like a variable read). Identical semantics in the editor preview and exported games: the darkness pass runs after particles and before the HUD in both draw paths (menus stay readable), lights follow their objects automatically (collected at draw time), hidden/HUD objects never shine, and the export's offscreen canvas creation is guarded so vm-stubbed hosts skip the overlay instead of breaking the frame.
- **Design guard (caught by inspection, not by tests):** both runtimes' `normalizeObject` call `normalizeLight2d` for any object carrying a `light` record — but light3d objects carry their own `light` record shape (type/range, intensity up to 4), which 2D normalization would have rewritten (intensity clamped to 1, radius invented). The calls are now guarded away from 3D kinds/light3d, and Phase AR asserts a directional 3D light (intensity 2.5) survives export normalization untouched.
- Test-authoring fixes (mine, not engine bugs): (1) my shadowPolygon far-point check measured from the wrong corners — the module's silhouette pair is the left corners for a light on the left, and the far points sit at range from their own corners; (2) my export draw-path check assumed the 300x150 stub canvas size, but the export resizes the canvas to the 800x600 viewport, so the darkness fill is 800x600; (3) the pre-existing 3D-light smoke check clicked the Light button while the scene was still 2D — with the button now context-aware it must switch the scene to 3D first (and my lighting checks switch it back to 2D before creating the 2D light); (4) my lighting palette clicks first failed because the Blend Active panel check had left the properties panel over the palette — the section now restores the palette with the unconditional back-to-blocks click first.
- Tests: new `tests/light2d.test.js` (58 checks: module surface, factorySource, normalization incl. repairs, light collection incl. center math/enabled-skip/repairs, occluder filtering, shadow polygons incl. left/right/above lights + wraparound + inside-box null + hostile, projection incl. zoom, hexToRgba, drawDarkness on recording contexts incl. fill/punch/tint/shadow/stamp/skip/hostile, both action dispatches incl. all warns + holds + blank quiet, condition operators + quiet + hostile); `tests/export-boot.test.js` Phase AR (17 checks: export inlining, missing-module guard, setDarkness through real grid wiring, darknessLevel fires + reversed, setLight2d wiring + corrupt radius/color warns + holds + blank quiet, darknessLevel fires while paused, draw path paints darkness + light hole + shadow polygon through the module with an injected recording canvas, 3D light record untouched); all module-load sequences in export-boot.test.js plus fps-export/flagship updated for the new hard guard; `tests/flow.test.js` (darknessLevel → level); 14 new `tests/browser-smoke.js` checks (module loaded, all 3 palette blocks + Lighting category, Set Darkness placement + Darkness panel, Set Light placement + Object/Radius/Color/Light panel, Darkness Level placement + Darkness/Operator/Invert panel, Light button creates a light2d object + bulb glyph in a 2D scene, Inspector Lighting section, Scene Settings darkness slider writes scene.darkness). Renaming an animation repoints blend points bound to it (extended `propagateAnimationRename`); deleting an animation unbinds it from points; renaming a blend space repoints every `setBlend`/`stopBlend`/`blendActive` block (new `propagateBlendRename`).
- Three new blocks (index.html palette, auto-placed after the Animation State condition): **Set Blend** (action — Object + Blend + X + Y expression fields; sets the values and activates the blend; unknown blends warn + hold, blank stays quiet), **Stop Blend** (action — Object + Blend; deactivates, drawing returns to the normal animation), and the invertible **Blend Active** (condition — Object + Blend + Invert; 'level' trigger default in hydra-flow.js, evaluates while paused like a variable read). Identical semantics in the editor preview and exported games: fresh blend runtime per run/scene load, the frame clock advances inside the unpaused update (blends freeze while paused), clones start with no active blend, both runtimes delegate through the same hooks contract (warn → diagnostics, frame resolution → HydraAnim.getAnimation).
- **Drawing:** both runtimes' sprite draw paths ask `blendFrameList` first — while a blend is active its frames win over the playing animation; the winner draws fully, the runner-up at partial alpha (the crossfade), then the normal static-image fallback applies. A missing frame asset falls back to the static sprite, never a broken frame.
- Test-authoring fixes (mine, not engine bugs): (1) a typo'd `o._blends` in the export's drawObject (the parameter is `obj`) — caught by inspection before the suite ran; (2) my Phase AQ stop test first put a level-triggered Blend Active on the same frame as setBlend, so the stop ran on frame 1 — split into two sessions (activate + clock advance, then a chained set-then-stop propagation); (3) my array-list bulk update missed `apModules` (no trailing comma) — fixed, and deduped 13 pre-existing double `hydra-statemachine.js` entries from cycle 37's bulk update; (4) my first Phase AQ unknown-blend check read the diagnostic record's `.message` wrong — aligned with Phase AP's `.some(d => /.../.test(d.message))` pattern.
- Tests: new `tests/blendspace.test.js` (40 checks: module surface, factorySource, normalization incl. dedupe/suffixing/fps repair/point coercion, lookups, setBlend activate/values/unknown-warn+hold/blank-quiet/hostile, stopBlend blank-stops-active/hostile, updateBlend clock accumulation/advance/deleted-blend/deactivate/hostile, blendFrameList exact-hit/midway-crossfade/nearer-weight/wrap-per-length/single-point/missing-animations/no-resolver/hostile); `tests/export-boot.test.js` Phase AQ (17 checks: export inlining, missing-module guard, setBlend through real grid wiring + values stored, blendActive fires + reversed silent, unknown warns + holds, blank quiet, clock advances while active, chained set-then-stop deactivates + blendActive silent + zero diagnostics, blendActive fires while paused, blended frame list through the export); all module-load sequences in export-boot.test.js plus fps-export/flagship updated for the new hard guard; `tests/flow.test.js` (blendActive → level); 11 new `tests/browser-smoke.js` checks (module loaded, all 3 palette blocks, Set Blend placement + Object/Blend/X/Y panel, Blend Active placement + Blend/Invert panel, Inspector Blend Spaces section for sprites, + Add blend space card with Speed (fps), + Add direction row with Animation + X + Y). Full suite ALL green.

## 2026-11-04 — Cycle 37 (improve): Advanced batch 1 — animation state machines COMPLETED (+ Inspector Animations wiring fix)

- Implemented the first Advanced batch: **animation state machines** — named states bound to sprite animations + playback speed, driven by ordinary blocks. New shared pure module `hydra/hydra-statemachine.js` (HydraStateMachine, `factorySource` per the hydra-*.js pattern, inlined into exports via `buildPlayableHtml`, hard load guard in player-runtime.js): `normalizeStates` (blank names filled, duplicate names suffixed, bad speeds repair to 1, corrupt entries dropped), `stateNames`/`getState`, `createRuntime`/`currentState`, `setState(rt, obj, name, hooks)` (blank names a quiet no-op, unknown names warn + hold, never throws), `enterInitial` (unknown initial warns once at start). Nothing ever throws; hostile input degrades to safe defaults.
- `HydraAnim` gained a **playback-speed multiplier**: `createAnimState` carries `speed: 1`, `play`/`setAnimation` reset it to 1, new `setSpeed` export sanitizes (non-finite/<=0 repairs to 1), and `update` divides the frame time by the speed. Backward compatible — speed 1 is byte-identical to the old timing.
- **Inspector:** new **Animation States** section under Animations — add/rename/delete states, per-state animation select + speed (0.1-8), initial-state select. Renaming an animation repoints every bound state (extended the existing `propagateAnimationRename`); renaming a state repoints every `setAnimState`/`animStateIs` block + the initial setting (new `propagateStateRename`); deleting an animation unbinds it from states.
- **BUG FIX (found while scoping this cycle): the Inspector Animations section was dead code since the animation feature shipped** — `appendAnimationEditor` existed in script.js but nothing ever called it (verified: the original feature commit added the function with no call site, and no commit since added one), so users could not build the animation lists the Play Animation block's own note tells them to build. It is now wired into `showObjectProperties` for sprite-drawn objects (not text/panels/tilemaps/3D kinds/lights — the only kinds whose frames actually render), which the state machine depends on.
- Two new blocks (index.html palette, auto-placed after Animation Finished): **Set Animation State** (action — Object + State; enters the state and auto-plays its animation at the state's speed; unknown states warn + hold, blank stays quiet) and the invertible **Animation State** (condition — Object + State + Invert; 'level' trigger default in hydra-flow.js, evaluates while paused like a variable read). Identical semantics in the editor preview and exported games: fresh machine state per run/scene load, initial state entered at start (wins over play-at-start), spawn clones start in the initial state, both runtimes delegate through the same hooks contract (warn → diagnostics, play → HydraAnim.play + setSpeed).
- Test-authoring fixes (mine, not engine bugs): (1) two wrong expectations in the new unit test (my input had 7 entries not 6; the null-animation entry was index 4 not 3); (2) a tautological throwing-hook check rewritten as a real never-escapes assertion; (3) an edit-tool multi-match landed a test block in the wrong section — moved; (4) misread a grep and thought the module-load bulk update had missed lines — verified the insertions were correct; (5) the two new Inspector smoke checks first used case-sensitive regexes against CSS-uppercased section titles ("ANIMATION STATES") — the sections had been rendering all along; matched case-insensitively.
- Tests: new `tests/statemachine.test.js` (35 checks: module surface, factorySource, normalization incl. dedupe/suffixing/speed repair, lookups, runtime reads, setState enter/idempotent/no-animation/blank-quiet/unknown-warn+hold/no-hooks/hostile/throwing-hook, enterInitial incl. unknown-warns-once); `tests/anim.test.js` (13 new speed checks: defaults, setSpeed sanitizing, play/setAnimation reset, 2x frame math, hostile direct write); `tests/export-boot.test.js` Phase AP (22 checks: export inlining, missing-module guard, initial state entered + playing at load, setAnimState through real wiring incl. animation + 1.5x speed, animStateIs fires + reversed, unknown warns + holds, blank quiet, animation-less flag state, paused evaluation, zero diagnostics); all module-load sequences in export-boot.test.js plus fps-export/flagship updated for the new hard guard; `tests/flow.test.js` (animStateIs → level); 10 new `tests/browser-smoke.js` checks (module loaded, both palette blocks, placement + Object/State and State/Invert panels, Inspector Animations section now reachable for sprites, Animation States section with Initial state, + Add state creates a card with Animation + Speed). Full suite ALL green.

## 2026-11-04 — Cycle 36 (improve): P4 item 35 — Text scripting API COMPLETED

- Implemented the text scripting API: **JavaScript for power users alongside the visual blocks**. New shared pure module `hydra/hydra-scriptapi.js` (HydraScriptApi, `factorySource` per the hydra-*.js pattern, inlined into exports via `buildPlayableHtml`, hard load guard in player-runtime.js): builds the documented `hs` object over host hooks — variables, wrapped live objects (x/y/cx/cy, move/teleport/setText/show/hide/setColor/setOpacity/setRotation/destroy/dist; visibility goes through the host's hidden set so scripts and Show/Hide blocks agree), input actions, timers (frozen-clock reads), audio (name-to-assetId lookup, buses, music), scenes (name-to-id lookup, deferred switch), the `camera` object, particles (block preset names resolved case-insensitively), save slots, pause/profiler flow, tiles, math helpers, `dt` frame seconds, and `log`.
- `runAction` compiles statements; `evalCondition` compiles expressions (falling back to statement bodies with an explicit `return`). Compile errors warn once per code string (cached, 256-entry cap); runtime errors are caught and warned (deduped by host diagnostics) and never break the frame; blank code is a quiet no-op; hostile/missing hooks degrade to safe defaults. Nothing ever throws.
- Two new **Code** blocks (block-catalog.js, auto-added to the palette): **Run Script** (action, multi-line code field with an example placeholder) and the invertible **Script Check** (condition, expression field + Invert; edge trigger default, evaluates while paused like musicPlaying since it is code, not physics). Identical semantics in the editor preview and exported games — both runtimes implement the full hook contract (`scriptApiHooks()` in script.js, `scriptApiHooks(state)` in player-runtime.js), mirroring each other case by case.
- Real bugs caught by design review (mine): (1) the wrapper first read/wrote `obj.visible`, but both runtimes track show/hide in a hidden Set — scripts would have disagreed with the Show/Hide block; now goes through `isHidden`/`setHidden` hooks; (2) the export's `liveObjects` first excluded hidden objects, so a script could never unhide a menu — now uses `named(state, n, true)` to match the preview.
- Test-authoring fixes (mine, not engine bugs): (1) my compile-cache check first expected two warnings for two runs of bad code — the module now warns once per cached failure (both runtimes also dedupe); (2) my dist expectation used wrong center math (88/3, not 104/17); (3) the Phase AO session needed a `performance` stub on the fake frame clock (the export runtime assumes `performance.now()` exists, like the rest of the player); (4) two edit-tool multi-match mishaps (a removed `let tt` in the Phase AI starter, a misplaced smoke-test block) — both caught by inspection and repaired before the suite ran.
- Tests: new `tests/scriptapi.test.js` (97 checks: module surface, factorySource, statement/expression/statement-mode evaluation, error policy, warn-once caching, the full API over stub hooks incl. wrapper getters/setters and hidden-set visibility, hostile null hooks); `tests/export-boot.test.js` Phase AO (17 checks: export inlining, missing-module guard, Run Script through real grid wiring, Script Check edge + reversed, corrupt-code warn-once without breaking siblings, blank quiet, runtime-error-false, timer/save/dt hooks, scripts evaluate while paused); all module-load sequences in export-boot.test.js plus fps-export/flagship updated for the new hard guard; 6 new `tests/browser-smoke.js` checks (module loaded, Code palette blocks, placement, code textarea, code + Invert fields). Full suite ALL green.

## 2026-11-04 — Cycle 35 (improve): P4 item 34 — Profiling overlay COMPLETED

- Implemented the profiling overlay: **FPS + frame time (rolling 60-frame average), 2D sprite draws, 3D triangle draws, live particles, and live objects**, drawn as a small dark corner panel on top of the HUD pass in both the editor preview and exported games. New shared pure module `hydra/hydra-profile.js` (HydraProfile, `factorySource` per the hydra-*.js pattern, inlined into exports via `buildPlayableHtml`, hard load guard in player-runtime.js like the other modules): `createProfiler`/`isVisible`/`show`/`hide`/`toggle`, `frame(prof, nowMs)` (host passes its own clock so the module stays DOM-free; first call arms, later calls push real deltas into a 60-sample ring), `noteDraws` (sanitized per-frame counts), `stats` (plain record: fps, frameMs, draws2d, tris3d, particles, objects), `drawOverlay` (no-op when hidden or the ctx cannot draw text; hostile contexts caught so a broken canvas never breaks the frame). Visibility is runtime session state on `state.profiler` / `previewState.profiler` — never saved into project JSON, survives scene switches (a debug overlay, like the diagnostics panel, is not game state).
- **Counts plumbing:** both runtimes feed real frame timestamps into `frame()` every frame; the export's `draw()` and the preview's `drawGame()` count 2D draws around every `drawAny`/`drawOne` call, take the 3D tri count from `Hydra3d.renderScene`'s **new tri-count return** (backward compatible — old callers ignore it; early-failure reads 0 via the hoisted `draw` var), and read live particles from `particles.particles.length` plus live objects from the alive filter. The overlay paints last, after the HUD pass.
- **Four new Game Flow blocks** (block-catalog.js, auto-added to the palette): **Show Profiler**, **Hide Profiler**, **Toggle Profiler** (one block for a debug key), and the invertible **Profiler Visible** condition (reads overlay state only — not world-dependent, so it evaluates normally while paused like musicPlaying; edge trigger default, matching Game Paused). Wired into both runtimes with identical semantics: export dispatch cases + `isCondition` registration; preview dispatch cases + `evaluateProfilerVisibleCondition`. The preview's `isConditionRuntimeType`/`isActionRuntimeType` are catalog-driven so no preview registry change was needed.
- **Preview toolbar:** new gauge **Profiler** button next to Restart. It flips the same runtime state the blocks use, and its `active` style syncs every frame in `drawGame()` so block-driven changes still show the right button state — the no-blocks on-ramp for playtesting.
- Test-authoring fixes (mine, not engine bugs): (1) my first overlay unit check regexed `/FPS 5\d/` against a real "FPS 60" — fixed to `/FPS 6\d/`; (2) my bulk module-load script missed the three export-phase module arrays ending in `hydra-webexport.js`/`desktopexport.js`/`mobileexport.js` instead of `player-runtime.js`, plus the arrays ending in `'hydra-3d.js']` with player-runtime on the next line — all caught by the hard guard failing loudly and fixed with targeted inserts; (3) `profilerVisible` needed registering in the export's hardcoded `isCondition` list (the preview is catalog-driven).
- Tests: new `tests/profile.test.js` (30 checks: module surface, factorySource, visibility transitions incl. idempotence + missing-record safety, rolling average incl. 60-sample cap + spike resistance + backwards-time ignore, count sanitizing, stats record, overlay paint incl. hidden-paints-nothing/title/FPS/sprite-tri/particle-object lines/save-restore/hostile view size/missing ctx/throwing ctx); `tests/export-boot.test.js` Phase AN (11 checks: export inlines the module, missing-module guard throws the clear message, Show Profiler through real grid wiring + rolling FPS + draws2d=2 + silent, Profiler Visible fires + silent, Hide/Toggle through wiring + silent, inlined painter draws the panel on a recording ctx); all 53 module-load sequences in export-boot.test.js plus fps-export/flagship updated for the new hard guard; 8 new `tests/browser-smoke.js` checks (module loaded, all 4 palette blocks, Show Profiler + Profiler Visible placement, Profiler Visible Invert field, preview toolbar button, button lights up on click, dims on second click). Full suite ALL green.

## 2026-11-04 — Cycle 34 (improve): P4 3D5 — 3D lighting COMPLETED

- Implemented the 3D5 pillar and closed out the 3D pillar: **ambient, directional, and point lights as real objects, plus a simple shadow toggle**. The Create palette's Light button (previously a non-functional 2D sprite placeholder with an unwired Light component stub) now creates a true **light3d** object kind — invisible and non-physical, shown in the scene editor as a bulb glyph tinted by the light color with a type label (Point/Directional/Ambient, "(off)" when disabled) so it stays selectable and draggable.
- New shared-module lighting in hydra-3d.js (pure, inlined into exports via factorySource like the rest of the 3D module): `normalizeLight3d` (type/color/intensity/range≥0/enabled, idempotent), `migrateLegacyLight3d` (promotes pre-pillar sprite placeholders — archetype 'light3d' — carrying the legacy component's lightType/color/intensity over; called in both normalizeObject paths so editor and exports migrate identically), `collectLights3d` (enabled lights as plain records; directional lights aimed by Rot X / Rot Y with no rotation reproducing the classic fixed key light), `computeLightRGB` (per-triangle irradiance: ambient adds everywhere, directional × max(0,N·L), point × max(0,N·L) × linear falloff to range with range 0 = no falloff; channels clamped to the 1.15 shade headroom; scenes with lights but no ambient light keep a dim 0.25 white base so nothing goes pitch black — an Ambient light replaces it, even at intensity 0 for explicit darkness). `light3d` is deliberately NOT in THREE_D_KINDS: lights are excluded from 2D/3D physics, 2D draw passes, and both collision inspectors via the new `isLight3d` export.
- **Rendering:** renderScene collects lights once per frame. No lights → the legacy fixed-key-light path runs byte-identically (old projects render pixel-identically). With lights → every triangle gets per-channel lighting: the winding normal is flipped toward the camera first (the projector draws double-sided with no backface culling, so the visible side is what gets shaded), then flat triangles shade via new `shadeRGB3`/`shadeLitColor` and model textures via a multiply composite overlay (white = no-op) so colored lights tint textures exactly like flat shading. **Simple blob shadows:** `scene.shadows3d` draws a soft dark ellipse under each non-light 3D object on the horizontal ground plane y = `scene.shadowGroundY` (defaults to the bottom of the world), alpha fading with height; drawn after the grid, before geometry (documented cheap-shadow limit: no wrapping, drawn-before-geometry ordering).
- **New 3D block: Set 3D Light** (17 total — Object/Light name + Type + Color + Intensity + Range + On/Off; bad type/color/intensity/range/mode warn and hold, naming a non-light warns, blank names stay quiet, hostile input never throws). Day/night cycles, flickering torches, and muzzle flashes are now plain block recipes.
- **Editor:** Inspector 3D Transform section shared with lights (Z + Rot X/Y/Z; depth size and the 3D Collider subsection hidden for lights) plus a new **Light** section (Type select, color picker, Intensity, Range, Enabled checkbox, plain-language notes on aiming directionals and the ambient base rule); the sprite branch (image upload, color fill, Static/Physics toggles), Menus & HUD section, and 2D Collision section all skip lights. Scene Settings (3D scenes) gained the **3D Shadows** switch + **Shadow Ground Y** field, synced through the existing camera-fields handler. The in-editor 3D viewport, preview, and exports all collect lights and honor the shadow switch.
- Test-authoring fixes (mine, not engine bugs): (1) my first directional expectation used an unnormalized test normal (N·L = 1.033, not 1) — now uses H.vnorm; (2) my point-light expectation used the wrong light center (54px-wide light at posX 0 centers at 27, not 5) — fixed the fixture to center (5,5,200); (3) the shared `hooks()` test helper accumulates warnings on the function object, not the returned hooks — my set3dLight tests now pass their own warn collectors.
- Tests: tests/3d.test.js (20 new checks: isLight3d, normalizeLight3d defaults/repairs, normalizeObject3d light fields, legacy migration incl. component mapping, collectLights3d enabled-skip + directional default aim, computeLightRGB base/ambient-replace/directional N·L/point falloff+cutoff+range-0/hostile, shadowParams3d above/below/light/non-3D/hostile, renderScene red-ambient paints all 12 fills red while legacy does not, renderScene camera-side normal flip for double-sided triangles, shadow pass draws ellipses only when enabled + safe without ctx.ellipse, set3dLight write + 5 bad-data warn+hold + non-light warn + blank quiet + hostile); tests/export-boot.test.js Phase AM (14 checks: lighting API inlined, light normalizes, no 2D collision, zero-diagnostic lit render, set3dLight rewrite through real grid wiring, corrupt color warns + holds, legacy placeholder migrates, shadows3d renders clean); 10 new tests/browser-smoke.js checks (Set 3D Light palette + placement + panel fields, Light create button, bulb glyph without thumbnail, Light inspector section with Type select, collider/2D-collision/physics hidden for lights, Scene Settings shadow switch + ground Y writing scene.shadows3d, Hydra3d lighting API loaded). Full suite ALL green.
- Docs: user-guide 3D basics (16 → 17 blocks, Set 3D Light bullet, honest-limits rewritten — lighting now exists) + new "3D lighting" section (three light kinds, the two lighting rules, day/night + torch-flicker + muzzle-flash recipes, blob-shadow explanation and honest limits); block-reference 3D (16) → 3D (17) with the Set 3D Light entry (kept honest by tests/docs.test.js); ROADMAP 3D5 checked off (also checked off 3D1/3D2 parents — their "Remaining" items were all complete); CHANGELOG entry added.

## 2026-11-04 — Cycle 33 (improve): P4 3D4 — 3D physics basics COMPLETED

- Implemented the 3D4 pillar: **gravity, box/sphere 3D colliders, ground detection, and simple collision response in the editor preview and exported games**. New shared-module step `Hydra3d.updatePhysics3d(objects, dt, getPhysics)` in hydra-3d.js (pure, inlined into exports via factorySource like the rest of the 3D module): per-object gravity/friction/maxSpeed read from the shared `obj.physics` fields — one Inspector Physics panel drives both 2D and 3D — 3D velocity on `obj.vel3x/vel3y/vel3z` (zeroed per scene load/preview run), per-object collider on `obj.physics3d` { shape: 'box'|'sphere', solid } normalized by `normalizeObject3d` (old projects keep working), axis-aligned box colliders (w/h/depth box) and sphere colliders (radius = half the smallest side), minimum-penetration-axis push-out between dynamics (Physics on + not static) and solids (static or Physics off) with both needing to be solid to block (non-solid = ghost, passes through), velocity killed along the contact normal on the dominant axis (the 2D convention) so objects rest on floors and slide along walls, and `obj._onGround3d` set while a solid wall is below. Hosts call it inside the unpaused update, so 3D physics freezes while paused exactly like 2D physics.
- Four new **3D** blocks (16 total): **Set 3D Gravity** (action — writes the shared physics.gravity field and enables physics, mirroring the 2D Set Gravity block; the Inspector panel and the block can never disagree), **Set 3D Velocity** (action — VX/VY/VZ direct; a negative VY is a jump), **3D On Ground** (condition — true while a 3D object rests on a solid surface, the 3D On Floor), **3D Collision** (condition — shape-aware collider overlap between two 3D objects, the 3D Collision; raw overlap, ignores solidity). Both conditions are world-dependent: they report the paused freeze through a new optional `hooks.paused` (wired in both runtimes' threeDActionHooks) instead of stale state, mirroring 2D collision/onFloor, and both default to 'level' trigger mode in hydra-flow.js. Bad expressions warn and skip (the 3D convention); blank names stay quiet.
- **Inspector:** the 3D Transform section gained a **3D Collider** subsection (Box/Sphere shape select + Solid checkbox). The inert 2D Collision section no longer renders for 3D kinds — it edited fields that do nothing for 3D objects, so showing both would lie about which one works. (The shared Static/Physics toggles + Physics panel were already visible for 3D objects via the sprite branch and now genuinely drive 3D physics.)
- Test-authoring fix (mine, not an engine bug): `pairOverlap3d('x', b)` fabricated a collider for the non-object string instead of returning null — the module now rejects non-object inputs up front (the 2D "bad objects stay quiet" spirit).
- Tests: tests/3d.test.js (17 new checks: normalizePhysics3d defaults/repairs, collider3dOf box/sphere, pairOverlap3d min-axis/sphere-sphere/box-sphere/hostile/self, updatePhysics3d gravity-friction-maxSpeed integration, box landing on a floor with exact rest position, sphere landing, wall side-push, non-solid pass-through, dynamics ignoring each other, hostile never-throws, both action dispatches incl. warnings, both condition dispatches incl. pause reporting); tests/export-boot.test.js Phase AL (10 checks: gravity drop + real landing + _onGround3d, onGround3d fires through real wiring, set3dVelocity Z step, collision3d fires on real touch + reversed fires while apart, corrupt gravity warns + holds, blank conditions quiet, zero diagnostics); tests/flow.test.js (onGround3d/collision3d → level); 9 new tests/browser-smoke.js checks (all 4 palette blocks, Set 3D Gravity placement + Object/Gravity panel, 3D On Ground placement + Object/Invert panel, inspector 3D Collider section with shape + solid, 2D Collision section hidden for 3D). Full suite ALL green.
- Docs: user-guide 3D basics (12 → 16 blocks, new bullets, honest-limits updated — 3D objects now have their own physics) + new "3D physics" section with the 3D platformer recipe (gravity → land, On Ground + Just pressed → Set 3D Velocity jump, static walls, 3D Collision pickups); block-reference 3D (12) → 3D (16) with full entries (kept honest by tests/docs.test.js); ROADMAP 3D4 checked off; CHANGELOG entry added.
