## 2026-11-04 — Cycle 32 (improve): P4 3D3 — composable character controllers COMPLETED

- Implemented the 3D3 pillar: **FPS mouse-look + WASD and third-person orbit as documented block recipes from primitive blocks — no black-box controller component**, per the roadmap. Six new blocks, all ordinary blocks a beginner can open and rebuild:
  - **Aim 3D Camera** (3D action): keeps the camera where it is and re-aims its look-at point from yaw/pitch degrees. Yaw 0 = into the screen (-Z), positive yaw turns right (+X); pitch 0 = level, positive pitch looks down (+Y, screen-down); non-positive look distance falls back to 500. Unlike Set 3D Camera it never ends a Follow 3D Object — that is the composition: Follow parks the camera on the player's head, Aim points it every frame.
  - **Move 3D Facing** (3D action): Forward/Strafe steps along the yaw heading (forward = (sin yaw, -cos yaw), character-right = (cos yaw, sin yaw) in XZ — verified against the engine's right-handed x-right/y-down/z-toward-viewer frame); same yaw convention as Aim 3D Camera so WASD and mouse-look always agree.
  - **Capture Mouse** / **Release Mouse** (Input actions): pointer lock for mouse-look. Browsers only grant it from a real click, so the recipe starts from Object Clicked; a failed request warns once (deduped by the runtimes) with the click fix. Esc always releases (browser behavior).
  - **Read Mouse Delta** (Input action): copies this frame's accumulated mouse movement into two game variables. Both runtimes accumulate pointer-lock movementX/Y while captured, cursor travel otherwise, and reset at the end of each frame (after scripts run). Blank names warn and skip.
  - **Mouse Pointer Captured** (Input condition): true while pointer lock holds; 'level' trigger default in hydra-flow.js (input-state read like cursorPosition), evaluates while paused; invert it to catch Esc and show the pause menu.
- New pure helpers in hydra-3d.js (`yawPitchLookAt`, `facingDelta`, exported), inlined into exports via the existing factorySource; the module never touches the DOM — pointer lock, lock state, delta accumulation, and variable writes arrive through documented host hooks (`captureMouse`/`releaseMouse`/`pointerLocked`/`readMouseDelta`/`setVar`), implemented in both runtimes.
- Test-authoring fixes (mine, not engine bugs): (1) my first yawPitchLookAt fallback expectation had the sign wrong ([1,2,3] + 500·[0,0,-1] = z -497, not 503); (2) the corrupt-aim export check first expected the action to hold, but the expression evaluator coerces unknown names to 0 with a warning by engine design (the batch-7 lesson) — the check now asserts that documented coercion (yaw junk → 0, tz = 420, one diagnostic); (3) the delta-reset export check first re-read the variable (gameStart is edge, so the variable isn't rewritten) — it now asserts `state.mouseDelta` resets to 0 at frame end; (4) my new smoke checks first ran while the properties panel was still open (the block selector hides behind it), so the palette clicks failed "not visible" — the checks now click back-to-blocks before touching the palette, matching the existing pattern.
- Tests: tests/3d.test.js (15 new checks: aim math incl. yaw-90/pitch-90/fallback/hostile, facing deltas incl. strafe-at-yaw-90, aim dispatch + never-clears-follow, bad-yaw warn+hold, facing dispatch + bad-forward warn+hold, capture success-silent/failure-warn/throw-safe, release throw-safe, readMouseDelta write + blank-warn + missing-hooks, pointerCaptured pass/quiet/throw-safe); tests/export-boot.test.js Phase AK (9 checks: aim re-aims the live camera, facing steps, delta write + frame reset, captured quiet-unlocked/fires-locked + silent capture, missing-API warn with the click fix, corrupt-yaw coercion, zero diagnostics on clean paths); tests/flow.test.js (mousePointerCaptured → level); browser-smoke checks (all 6 palette blocks, Aim 3D Camera + Read Mouse Delta placement and panel fields, Hydra3d helpers loaded). Full suite ALL green.
- Docs: user-guide "3D basics" honest-limits paragraph updated (controllers no longer pending; "All nine" → "All twelve"); new "3D character controllers" section with the full FPS recipe (click-to-play → Capture Mouse; Every Frame → Read Mouse Delta → yaw/pitch vars with Clamp Number; Follow 3D Object (0,-40,0) + Aim 3D Camera; named WASD actions → Move 3D Facing; inverted Mouse Pointer Captured → Esc menu) and the third-person orbit recipe (re-run Follow 3D Object every frame with yaw-rotated offsets); block-reference 3D (10) → 3D (12) and Input (11) → Input (15) with full entries (kept honest by tests/docs.test.js); ROADMAP 3D3 checked off; CHANGELOG entry added.
- Next cycle: 3D4 — 3D physics basics (gravity, box/sphere 3D colliders, ground detection, simple collision response in preview and exports).

## 2026-11-04 — Cycle 31 (improve): P4 3D2 batch 2 — texture mapping COMPLETED

- Implemented the second slice of the 3D-assets pillar: **texture mapping**. `hydra/hydra-gltf.js` now reads each material's `baseColorTexture` — TEXCOORD_0 UVs (floats plus normalized integer types de-normalized per spec), embedded image bytes (bufferView into the GLB binary chunk or data: URIs; separate-file image URIs are skipped with flat colors, not an error), 8 MB per-image cap, plain-language failures, never throws. Parsed triangles carry `uv` ([[u,v]x3], glTF top-left origin) and `tex` (texture index or -1); `fitModelToBox` carries `uvs`/`tex` parallel to tris. New `bytesToBase64` export so hosts can turn texture bytes into `<img>` data URLs without btoa (vm-sandbox safe).
- **Projector** (hydra-3d.js): textured triangles sample the image with an affine texture-to-screen transform (`ctx.transform` + clip + `drawImage`), darkened by the same flat-shade light factor and washed by the material tint (white = no-op wash). Degenerate UVs, missing/undecoded images, or no `Image` support fall back to the flat color path — never a broken frame. Mixed meshes (textured + flat tris) draw correctly.
- **Hosts:** the editor (`modelTextureImages`) and exported games (`modelTextures`) decode each texture image once per asset via `Image`; ready images attach to the fitted mesh as `texImages`, and a decode landing drops that asset's mesh-cache entries so the next frame re-fits with the texture. Until then the model draws flat. The mesh cache is also dropped on asset replace/delete.
- **Editor UI:** Inspector gained a **Use model texture** checkbox (on by default — a textured .glb shows its own skin; off shows flat colors; thumbnails honor it); the asset manager shows a `texture` tag on textured models; the stale "Textures are not supported yet" note is gone. New **3D** block **Use Model Texture** (Object + Texture On/Off select; bad modes warn and skip, the 3D convention) wired through `apply3dAction` in both runtimes — 3D blocks are now 10.
- Tests: `tests/gltf.test.js` (15 new checks: texture record extraction, byte identity, per-tri UVs/tex, quad UV corners, normalized ushort UVs, missing TEXCOORD_0, bad texture index, external-uri skip, oversize skip, data-URI image, bytesToBase64 round trip, hostile texture tables); `tests/3d.test.js` (10 new: useTexture normalize default, useModelTexture on/off + bad-mode warn, drawImage path, useTexture=false flat, missing-image flat, degenerate-UV flat, undecoded-image flat, mixed mesh, hostile meshes); `tests/export-boot.test.js` Phase AJ (8 checks: texture records through the export, uvs/tex parallel, image decode + texImages attach, Use Model Texture wiring off, corrupt mode warns + holds, no-Image-support flat fallback, zero diagnostics); 10 new `tests/browser-smoke.js` checks (synthesized textured .glb upload in-browser with a real canvas-painted 4x4 PNG → texture badge in the asset manager, picker lists it, image decodes in the editor, thumbnail canvas paints red/green/blue quads, Inspector checkbox, Use Model Texture in palette + places + panel fields). Full suite ALL green.
- Docs: user-guide 3D-models paragraph (texture behavior + honest limits) and the no-textures limit updated; block-reference 3D (9) → 3D (10) with the Use Model Texture entry (kept honest by `tests/docs.test.js`); ROADMAP 3D2 annotated with the batch-2 completion; CHANGELOG entry added.
- Next cycle: 3D3 — composable character controllers (FPS mouse-look + WASD and third-person follow as documented block recipes from primitive 3D blocks).

## 2026-11-04 — Cycle 30 (improve): P4 3D2 batch 1 — 3D model assets (GLB/GLTF upload) COMPLETED

- Implemented the first slice of the 3D-assets pillar. **New shared pure module `hydra/hydra-gltf.js`** (HydraGltf, `factorySource` per the hydra-*.js pattern, inlined into exports via `buildPlayableHtml`, hard load guard in player-runtime.js like the other modules): self-contained GLB 2.0 parser (chunk walking, DataView accessor reads for all 6 component types, indexed + non-indexed geometry, node TRS/matrix transforms baked to world space, per-primitive material base colors, glTF Y-up flipped to HydraScript Y-down so authored models stand upright, 20k-triangle cap with `truncated: true`), embedded-buffer `.gltf` support (external-`.bin` .gltf rejected with a plain-language "upload the .glb" message), self-contained base64/UTF-8 decoders (no atob/TextDecoder assumption for vm sandboxes), and `fitModelToBox` (scales the parsed model into the object's w/h/d box, returns hydra-3d's {verts, tris, colors} mesh shape). Nothing ever throws; hostile input returns `{ok:false, error}` with a plain-language message.
- **Model 3D object kind** (`model3d`, now the 5th entry in `THREE_D_KINDS`): Create palette button + Add Entity option, 120-unit cube defaults, live model thumbnail + 3D badge in the scene editor, inert to 2D physics/collision like the other 3D kinds. hydra-3d.js draws model3d through a new `resolveModel(obj)` hook on `renderScene` — the host supplies a fitted mesh (or null); missing asset / failed parse / no hook renders an honest placeholder box in the object's color, never a broken frame. The draw loop gained per-triangle material colors multiplied by the new `materialColor` tint (white = no-op, so old projects render pixel-identically).
- **Editor wiring:** Assets tab accepts `.glb`/`.gltf` (extension sniffing — File types are unreliable for models), 3D Model kind with cube icon, usage tracking (`where:'model'`) and reference cleanup in hydra-universal.js; Inspector **Model** picker (model assets only, inline upload button) + **Material color (tint)** picker in the 3D Transform section; model parse/mesh caches with invalidation on replace/delete; the provider feeds the thumbnail canvas, the in-editor 3D viewport, and the preview render path. **Export wiring:** `state.modelCache`/`state.modelMeshCache` — data-URL assets parse synchronously, web-target `{file}` assets fetch once async and draw the placeholder until bytes arrive.
- **New 3D block: Set Model Tint** (Object + Tint color picker) — runtime control over the material tint; bad colors warn and hold (the 3D convention).

Y-flip/bounds/material color/node translation+scale/indexed+non-indexed, 8 hostile inputs, triangle cap, embedded/external .gltf, parseModelBytes dispatch, fitModelToBox incl. hostile); `tests/3d.test.js` (kinds 4→5, actions 7→8, normalize/tint/shadeRGB, hook/null/missing/throwing-provider render paths, tint application, setModelTint dispatch incl. warnings); `tests/export-boot.test.js` Phase AI (14 checks: export inlining, missing-module guard, model parses to ready + mesh fitted in the export, tint through real grid wiring, corrupt tint warns + holds, missing asset placeholder, corrupt bytes clean failure, collision disabled, zero diagnostics); all 25 inline + 19 array module-load sequences in export-boot.test.js plus fps-export/flagship updated for the new hard guard; 12 new `tests/browser-smoke.js` checks (HydraGltf loaded, all 9 palette blocks, Set Model Tint placement + panel, Model 3D create + thumbnail + badge, Inspector Model picker + tint field, real .glb upload synthesized in-browser → 3D Model asset row, picker lists the upload, 4-triangle colored mesh resolves, thumbnail paints the model). Full suite ALL green.

## 2026-11-04 — Cycle 29 (improve): P4 3D1 batch 2 — in-editor 3D viewport + 3D camera follow COMPLETED

- Implemented the second slice of the 3D pillar. **In-editor 3D viewport:** 3D scenes get a **Layout / 3D view** segmented toggle in the scene toolbar (hidden for 2D scenes; a 2D scene always falls back to Layout). 3D view renders the stage with the same Hydra3d projector Preview and exported games use, with the scene's actual camera3d — the true perspective picture, refreshed after every scene edit (object add/move/property change, bg color, camera edits, render-mode switch, scene switch). Layout stays the place-and-drag view; a hint bar explains the split. Scene Settings gained **3D Camera Position / Look-At / FOV** fields (clearing a field drops that axis back to default framing) plus a **Reset to frame scene** button. The Inspector's 3D Transform note now points at the toolbar toggle instead of "hit Preview".
- **Camera follow blocks** (new shared-module support in hydra-3d.js, inlined into exports): `normalize3dFollow` + `tick3dCameraFollow` (parks the live camera at target center + offset, look-at at center; holds position when the target is gone; never throws) and two new 3D blocks — **Follow 3D Object** (Object + Offset X/Y/Z, defaults 0/-60/280 for a classic behind-and-above chase) and **Stop 3D Camera Follow**. Set 3D Camera clears an active follow (the 2D Move Camera rule). Follow state lives in runtime state (`previewState.camera3dFollow` / `state.camera3dFollow`, fresh per run and per scene), never in scene.camera3d, so it cannot leak into saved project JSON. The tick runs inside the unpaused update in both runtimes, so the follow freezes while paused like 2D camera motion (F4). Bad offset expressions warn and keep the old follow; blank name clears silently (the 2D followCamera convention).
- Test-authoring fixes (mine, not engine bugs): the Phase AH track checks first asserted absolute frame counts, but the harness's first pump doesn't advance scripts and the per-frame tick runs before scripts, so the camera lags a moving target by one frame — rewrote as relative bounds (within one frame of the target, target still advancing); the default-camera-z assertion needed a float epsilon (920 computes as 919.9999999999999).
- Real bug caught by visual QA (mine): the 3D view hid `.game-object` divs via stylesheet, but text objects carry an inline `display: block` from `applyTextStyle`, so they stayed visible over the 3D render. Fixed with `display: none !important` scoped to `.scene-3d-view` (the smoke check now asserts ALL object divs hide, not just the first). Screenshots verified: clean perspective render of all 4 primitives, camera Z field visibly dollies the view.
- Tests: `tests/3d.test.js` (7 action types now; normalize3dFollow sanitizing, tick parking/hold/hostile, follow/stop dispatch incl. warnings and blank-name clear, set3dCamera clears follow, old-host safety); `tests/export-boot.test.js` Phase AH (13 checks: inlined tick, exact park on a static target, tracking a moving target within one frame, stop holds the camera + clears state while the hero keeps moving, set3dCamera clears follow and its move wins, corrupt offset warns + holds + never crashes, zero diagnostics on clean paths); 12 new `tests/browser-smoke.js` checks (all 8 palette blocks, toggle appears for 3D / hides for 2D, 3D view canvas + hint + hidden divs + active button, Layout restores divs, camera fields write scene.camera3d, Reset re-frames, Follow/Stop placement + panel fields). Full suite ALL green.
- Docs: user-guide 3D basics gained the viewport + camera-fields + follow paragraphs (all eight blocks now); block-reference 3D (6) grew to 3D (8) with full entries + the Set 3D Camera follow-clear note (kept honest by `tests/docs.test.js`); ROADMAP 3D1 annotated with the batch-2 completion; CHANGELOG entry added.

## 2026-11-04 — Cycle 28 (improve): P4 3D1 batch 1 — 3D rendering foundation COMPLETED

- Implemented the first slice of the 3D pillar: **real 3D with a built-in CPU perspective renderer** (deliberate design call — see below). New shared pure module `hydra/hydra-3d.js` (Hydra3d, `factorySource` per the hydra-*.js pattern, inlined into exports via `buildPlayableHtml`, load guard in player-runtime.js like the other modules): vec3/Euler math, primitive mesh builders (box 12 tris, sphere 200, plane 2, cylinder 48), look-at perspective camera with y-down orientation matching the 2D canvas (X right, Y down, Z toward viewer — no "y is up" surprise), painter's-algorithm triangle sorting with flat shading from a fixed key light, a subtle reference grid on the z=0 plane, per-scene camera normalization (default frames the whole scene), and one-dispatch `apply3dAction`/`eval3dCondition` with the standard hooks contract (targets/num/warn + objectsByName/camera3d). Meshes are cached in a WeakMap keyed by object, so nothing leaks into saved project JSON. Nothing ever throws; hostile input produces safe fallbacks.
- **Design deviation from the roadmap (flagged for Ky'lin):** the roadmap says "Three.js-powered". I shipped a small built-in projector instead of vendoring Three.js (~600KB minified, no npm in this environment, and a hand-pasted copy would be unmaintainable). The user-visible goals are all met — per-scene 2D/3D toggle, 4 primitives, transform blocks, camera blocks, self-contained exports (single-file exports keep working with zero new dependencies). If Ky'lin wants real Three.js later (GLB models, real lighting), that is a 3D2/3D5 decision; the block API and scene model are designed to survive the swap.
- **Scene model:** `scene.renderMode` ('2d' default | '3d') + `scene.camera3d` {x,y,z,tx,ty,tz,fov}, normalized in hydra-universal.js (editor) and player-runtime.js (exports); old scenes keep renderMode '2d' and render pixel-identically. Scene Settings gained a Render Mode select (wired + synced on scene switch).
- **Objects:** 4 new kinds — box3d, sphere3d, plane3d, cylinder3d — in the Create palette (buttons + Add Entity dropdown) with per-kind colors and default depths. New fields `z` (depth position, 0 = screen plane), `depth` (size along Z), `rotX/rotY/rotZ` (degrees about the center); the rendered center is (posX + w/2, posY + h/2, z). Scene editor draws a live 3D thumbnail (fixed three-quarter camera) + a "3D" badge inside the object's div; drag still moves the 2D footprint. Inspector gained a **3D Transform** section (Z, Depth, Rot X/Y/Z) for 3D kinds. 3D objects are inert to the 2D physics/collision system in both runtimes (collision disabled at normalization + physics-step guards).
- **Blocks** (new **3D** category, #0ea5e9): **Move 3D** (dX/dY/dZ deltas), **Set 3D Position** (teleports the CENTER), **Set 3D Rotation** (absolute degrees), **Rotate 3D** (adds degrees — Every Frame + Rot Y 2 is the showcase spin), **Set 3D Camera** (camera XYZ + look-at XYZ, mutates the live camera), **3D Distance** (condition: center-to-center distance vs value, operators <=/>/>=/</==/!=, Invert; 'level' trigger default in hydra-flow.js, evaluates while paused, quiet on blank/missing names like the timer/pick convention).
- **Rendering:** editor preview and exported games both branch on renderMode — 3D scenes draw the bg color, the Hydra3d 3D pass, then 2D non-HUD objects as a flat overlay and the HUD pass last (menus/score bars keep working in 3D scenes). `state.camera3d`/`previewState.camera3d` is refreshed per scene load/preview run; Set 3D Camera mutates it live.
- Test-authoring fixes (mine, not engine bugs): (1) first bulk module-load insert used an over-broad regex that duplicated array-literal lines into syntax errors — reverted and redone with a two-case script (sandbox-load lines vs array-list lines), which also caught and reverted an accidental edit to the Phase AF guard-test line; (2) rotXYZ(null) threw — module now coerces hostile points; (3) mesh builders produced NaN verts on hostile sizes — builders now sanitize dimensions; (4) the 3d unit-test `num` hook returned {ok:false,value:0} which the value-finiteness convention reads as ok — aligned with the layerfx hook shape; (5) two wrong expectations (80x80 box center math; reversed-operator logic); (6) smoke check used case-sensitive /3D Transform/ against CSS-uppercased innerText.
- Tests: new `tests/3d.test.js` (49 checks: module surface, vec/rotation math incl. axis directions, mesh tri counts, camera normalization/defaults, projection geometry incl. behind-camera null and depth ordering, shading, centerOf/distance3d, renderScene on a stub ctx incl. all four primitives and hostile scenes, all 5 action dispatches incl. warnings, condition dispatch incl. quiet paths, hostile input); `tests/export-boot.test.js` Phase AG (22 checks: export inlining, missing-module guard, 3D boot + normalized camera, set3dPosition/move/rotate/setRotation/set3dCamera writes, distance3d fire/quiet/reversed/paused, corrupt-data warnings + quiet + no crash, 3D physics exclusion, 2D-mode fallback, zero diagnostics on clean paths); `tests/flow.test.js` (distance3d defaults to level); 16 new `tests/browser-smoke.js` checks (module loaded, all 6 palette blocks, Move 3D / Set 3D Camera / 3D Distance placement + panel fields, 4 create buttons, Box 3D thumbnail + badge, 3D Transform inspector section, render-mode toggle writing scene.renderMode). Full suite ALL green.
- Docs: user-guide gained the "3D basics" section (incl. the honest limits of this slice); block-reference gained the 3D (6) section (kept honest by `tests/docs.test.js`); ROADMAP 3D1 annotated with the batch-1 completion + deviation note (item stays open for 3D2-3D5; next batches: in-editor 3D viewport, camera follow blocks); CHANGELOG entry added.
- Next cycle: 3D1 batch 2 — in-editor 3D viewport (true 3/4 view in the scene editor) and/or 3D camera follow blocks; then 3D2 (GLB/GLTF model upload).

## 2026-11-04 — Cycle 27 (improve): P4 item 37 batch 12 — layers and effects COMPLETED

- Implemented the twelfth and final batch of the block-catalog expansion workstream: **layers and effects**. Nine new **Object** blocks (block-catalog.js, auto-added to the palette): 4 layer actions — **Bring to Front** (layer = highest live layer + 1), **Send to Back** (layer = lowest live layer - 1), **Move Up a Layer** / **Move Down a Layer** (shift by the Steps expression, default 1; bad steps warn and hold); 1 layer condition — **Layer Check** (Object + Layer + Compare + Invert; fires while any live object with the name matches); 4 effect actions — **Set Blend Mode** (Normal/Add/Multiply/Screen/Overlay/Difference select), **Set Shadow** (color picker + Blur 0-64 + Offset X/Y), **Set Visual Effect** (None/Grayscale/Sepia/Invert/Brighten/Dim/Soft Blur select), **Clear Effects** (resets blend/shadow/filter).
- New shared pure module `hydra/hydra-layerfx.js` (HydraLayerFx, `factorySource` per the hydra-*.js pattern, inlined into exports via `buildPlayableHtml`, load guard in player-runtime.js like the other modules): `layerOf`/`bringToFront`/`sendToBack`/`shiftLayer` helpers, `blendCompositeOf`/`filterOf`/`shadowBlurOf` mappings, `applyObjectEffects(ctx, obj)` draw-time application (defensive — hostile fields collapse to no effect, never throws), and one-dispatch `applyLayerFxAction`/`evalLayerFxCondition` with the standard hooks contract (targets/allObjects/num/warn). Effects live on plain object fields (`blendMode`, `shadowColor`, `shadowBlur`, `shadowOffsetX/Y`, `visualEffect`), so they serialize with the project and need no normalization — old projects just have no effects.
- Identical semantics in the editor preview and exported games: both runtimes delegate through one dispatch each (preview: `resolveActionTargets`/`getAlivePreviewObjects` + `evalExprInfo`/pushDiagnostic; export: `targets(state, data)` context-aware + exprInfo/reportRuntimeDiagnostic), so picking works — Pick Nearest → Bring to Front hits only the picked object. The 6 draw paths (preview text/panel/sprite, export text/panel/object) call `applyObjectEffects` inside their existing save/restore, so blend/shadow/filter reset automatically and the no-effect case costs almost nothing. Layer Check joined the 'level' trigger-mode list in hydra-flow.js (state read like getVariable) and evaluates while paused.
- Three test-authoring fixes (mine, not engine bugs): (1) the first perl for inserting module-load lines used a sed-ERE pattern that silently matched nothing — redone in perl with a backreference on the sandbox variable; (2) the Phase AF grid first stacked the miss-pair at cells 60/61, but cell 61 is orthogonally adjacent to the firing condition at cell 1 (the batch-7 lesson) — moved to rows 0/3 like Phase AA; (3) the corrupt layerCheck first used a bad *expression* value and expected quiet — wrong, unknown names coerce to 0 with a warning by engine design, so the corrupt vector is the operator select (stays quiet), matching the physics-query batch convention.
- Tests: new `tests/layerfx.test.js` (49 checks: module surface, layerOf incl. null/corrupt, bringToFront/sendToBack incl. empty scenes, all-negative/all-positive layers, and hostile entries, shiftLayer incl. hostile delta, every blend/filter mapping + unknown fallbacks, shadowBlurOf 0-64 clamp, applyObjectEffects on a stub context incl. hostile fields, all 8 action dispatches incl. warnings, clearEffects reset, condition dispatch incl. blank-name quiet and corrupt-layer-as-0, hostile hooks never throw); `tests/export-boot.test.js` Phase AF (21 checks: export inlining, missing-module guard, bringToFront 1→6, sendToBack 5→-1, moveLayerUp by steps, lone moveLayerDown never runs, moveLayerDown shift, layerCheck fires/quiet, blend/shadow/filter writes, clearEffects reset, bad-steps warn + hold, corrupt-operator quiet, layerCheck fires while paused, zero diagnostics on clean paths); `tests/flow.test.js` (layerCheck defaults to level); 8 new `tests/browser-smoke.js` checks (module loaded, all 9 palette blocks in Object category, Bring to Front / Set Blend Mode / Set Shadow placement and panel fields). Full suite ALL green.
- Docs: user-guide Objects section gained the "Layers and effects" paragraph; block-reference Object (16) grew to Object (25) with full entries (kept honest by `tests/docs.test.js`); ROADMAP item 37 checklist layers-and-effects checked off — **the item-37 coverage checklist is now empty**; CHANGELOG entry added.

## 2026-11-04 — Cycle 26 (improve): P4 item 37 batch 11 — object picking and selection COMPLETED

- Implemented the eleventh batch of the block-catalog expansion workstream: **object picking and selection**. Three new **Object** conditions (block-catalog.js, auto-added to the palette): **Pick Nearest** (Object + Near object + Point X/Y — picks the nearest live object with the name to the Near object; leaving Near object blank measures from the X/Y point instead), **Pick Farthest** (same fields, picks the furthest), **Pick Random** (Object — picks one uniform random live object with the name). Picking rides on the existing execution-context mechanism: an action chained after a pick with the same Object name applies ONLY to the picked object (the action's Target picker already defaults to context mode) — enemy AI targeting, homing pickups, "destroy only the closest enemy".
- New shared pure module `hydra/hydra-pick.js` (HydraPick, `factorySource` per the hydra-*.js pattern, inlined into exports via `buildPlayableHtml`, load guard in player-runtime.js like the other modules): pure helpers `objectCenter`/`pickNearest`/`pickFarthest`/`pickRandom` plus `isPickCondition`/`evalPickCondition` dispatch, which returns `{ pass, context }` with the picked object in `context.obj1`. Nothing ever throws; ties resolve to the first in live order (deterministic); blank/missing object names, missing references, and bad point expressions keep the condition quiet (no per-frame warning spam — the timer-condition convention, since there is no sensible Invert for "not the nearest").
- Identical semantics in the editor preview and exported games: both runtimes delegate through `pickHooks()` (preview: `getObjectsByName` over `getAlivePreviewObjects` + `evalExprInfo`/pushDiagnostic; export: `named()` + `exprInfo`/reportRuntimeDiagnostic), mirroring the math/collections wiring. Trigger modes (hydra-flow.js): pickNearest/pickFarthest default to 'level' (spatial state reads, re-evaluated every frame as objects move); pickRandom stays 'edge' on purpose (fires once per appearance — flip to level for a fresh random pick every frame). All three evaluate while paused (the pick is stable on the frozen world), like the batch-8 physics queries.
- One test-authoring scare (mine, not an engine bug): my first pass at the module-load update script used an over-broad regex that rewrote `filename: 'hydra-math.js'` strings into garbage — caught immediately, the three test files were reverted and redone with a targeted script plus manual Phase AE re-insertion; the damage never reached a commit.
- Tests: new `tests/pick.test.js` (30 checks: module surface, objectCenter incl. null/corrupt, nearest/farthest incl. ties-by-order, empty/non-array/hostile lists, random distribution + coverage over 2000 rolls, full condition dispatch incl. point fallback, blank/missing-name quiet, missing-reference quiet, bad-expression quiet, hostile input never throws); `tests/export-boot.test.js` Phase AE (17 checks: export inlining, missing-module guard, nearest destroys only enemy C / farthest only B / point-fallback nearest to (400,0) is B, chained destroy applies only to the picked object, random fires once (edge) destroying exactly one, missing/blank names quiet with zero diagnostics, corrupt data quiet + no crash, picks fire while paused, zero diagnostics on clean paths); `tests/flow.test.js` (pickNearest/pickFarthest → level, pickRandom → edge); 8 new `tests/browser-smoke.js` checks (module loaded, all 3 palette blocks in Object category, Pick Nearest + Pick Random placement and panel fields). Full suite ALL green.
- Docs: user-guide Objects section gained the "Picking one object out of many" paragraph; block-reference Object (13) grew to Object (16) with full entries (kept honest by `tests/docs.test.js`); ROADMAP item 37 checklist object picking checked off; CHANGELOG entry added.

## 2026-11-04 — Cycle 25 (improve): P4 item 37 batch 10 — math/random COMPLETED

- Implemented the tenth batch of the block-catalog expansion workstream: **math/random**. Five new **Variables** blocks (block-catalog.js, auto-added to the palette): **Random Integer** (action: Into variable + Min + Max expressions — uniform whole number in [Min, Max] inclusive; fractional bounds shrink to the whole range, reversed bounds auto-swap), **Clamp Number** (action: Number variable + Minimum + Maximum — forces the variable into range, writes back in place), **Round Number** (action: Number variable + Round to select — Whole number / Down / Up / 1 decimal / 2 decimals — writes back; corrupt modes fall back to whole), **Random Pick** (action: From list + Into variable — copies a uniformly random item from a list variable; writes 0 when the list is empty or missing, the Get Array Item convention), **Number In Range** (condition: Value + Minimum + Maximum + Invert — true while the value sits in the inclusive range; state read defaulting to 'level' trigger mode in hydra-flow.js, evaluates while paused).
- New shared pure module `hydra/hydra-math.js` (HydraMath, `factorySource` per the hydra-*.js pattern, inlined into exports via `buildPlayableHtml`, load guard in player-runtime.js like the other modules): pure helpers `randomInteger`/`clampNumber`/`roundValue`/`inRange`, `isMathAction`/`isMathCondition`, and one-dispatch `applyMathAction`/`evalMathCondition` with the standard hooks contract (num/warn/lookupVar). Nothing ever throws; hostile input produces safe fallbacks. Blank variable/list names warn (deduped by the runtimes) instead of silently misbehaving; bad numbers warn and skip the action while the condition stays quiet; expression warnings surface through hooks.num by design; non-numeric variable contents are treated as 0.
- Identical semantics in the editor preview and exported games: both runtimes delegate through `mathHooks()` (preview: `evalExprInfo`/pushDiagnostic; export: `exprInfo`/reportRuntimeDiagnostic), mirroring the collections wiring. The four actions joined the export's action list via `isMathAction`; Number In Range via `isMathCondition`.
- Three test-authoring fixes (mine, not engine bugs): (1) my naive unit-test hook only understands plain numbers, so the in-range assertion uses a literal (the batch-6 lesson); (2) two corrupt-data export cells were placed as lone actions or behind a false condition and never fired — lone actions never execute, so the corrupt randomPick moved next to a firing gameStart row; (3) every player-runtime module-load sequence in export-boot.test.js (25 inline + 13 array-based + Phase AC's own list), fps-export.test.js, and flagship.test.js had to load hydra-math.js or the new load guard threw — exactly the batch-6 lesson repeating.
- Tests: new `tests/math.test.js` (50+ checks: module surface, uniform integer distribution over 6000 rolls, bound shrinking/swapping, clamp/round/inRange incl. reversed bounds, all four action dispatches incl. warnings, randomPick list preservation + empty/missing writes 0, read-only condition, hostile input); `tests/export-boot.test.js` Phase AD (17 checks: export inlining, missing-module guard, randomInteger/clamp/round writes, reversed-bound swap, randomPick from a pushed list + missing list writes 0 + source preserved, numberInRange firing/quiet/reversed while paused, corrupt-data warnings + quiet + no crash, zero diagnostics on clean paths); `tests/flow.test.js` (numberInRange defaults to level); 8 new `tests/browser-smoke.js` checks (module loaded, all 5 palette blocks, Random Integer / Random Pick / Number In Range placement and panel fields). ## 2026-11-04 — Cycle 23 (improve): P4 item 37 batch 8 — physics queries COMPLETED

- Implemented the eighth batch of the block-catalog expansion workstream: **physics queries**. Four new **Collision** conditions (block-catalog.js, auto-added to the palette): **Angle Between** (angle in degrees from one object's hitbox center to another's — 0 = directly right, 90 = down, -90 = up — compared with >=/<=/==/!=/>/<, invertible), **Line of Sight** (true when no collider blocks the straight line between two objects; the two endpoints' own colliders are excluded, anything else on the layer mask blocks), **Distance To Nearest** (distance to the closest object with a named group name, compared against an expression; quiet when the group is empty), **Moving Towards** (object's velocity has a positive component along the a->b direction AND speed exceeds a min-speed expression, so stationary objects never count; invertible).
- Five new shared pure helpers in `hydra/hydra-physics2d.js` (auto-included in the factorySource that exports inline): `objectCenter` (hitbox center, same math the runtimes use for distance checks, null-safe), `distanceBetweenObjects`, `angleBetweenObjects`, `speedOf`, `movingTowards`. Never throw; hostile input produces safe fallbacks.
- Identical semantics in the editor preview and exported games: both runtimes delegate to the shared helpers (preview evaluators in script.js, `evalAngleBetween`/`evalLineOfSight`/`evalNearestDistance`/`evalMovingTowards` in player-runtime.js); the four conditions joined the 'level' trigger-mode list in hydra-flow.js (state reads like distanceCheck's cousins). Angle/nearest/moving evaluate while paused; Line of Sight reports the freeze like raycastHit.
- Two test-authoring fixes (mine, not engine bugs): (1) objectCenter(null) centers on the hitbox default (0.5, 0.5), not (0, 0) — expectation corrected to the honest value; (2) the corrupt test used `minSpeed: 'junk'`, but the expression evaluator warns on unknown names by design (batch-7 lesson) — the corrupt test now uses corrupt selects with clean values.

New shared pure module `hydra/hydra-collections.js` (HydraCollections, `factorySource` per the hydra-*.js pattern, inlined into exports via `buildPlayableHtml`, load guard in player-runtime.js like the other modules): array ops (push/pop/get/set/insert/remove-at/remove-value/clear/length/contains/indexOf/join/sort/shuffle/sum-average-min-max), structure ops (get/set/has/remove/clear/keys), JSON bridge (parse/stringify, circular-safe), block value resolution across five kinds (number expression / text / true / false / deep-copy-of-variable), and read-only condition dispatch. Nothing ever throws; hostile input produces safe fallbacks. 0-based indexes, -1 is the last item, out-of-range reads give 0, Set Array Item auto-grows with 0-fill capped at 10,000, field names are trimmed, blank variable/field/store names warn instead of silently misbehaving.

## 2026-11-04 — Main agent: P4 item 36 (flow semantics) COMPLETED

- **HydraFlow** (`hydra/hydra-flow.js`): new shared pure module (factorySource pattern) defining execution flow through puzzle connectors. API: `adjacentIndexes`, `flowEdges`, `defaultTriggerMode`, `triggerModeOf`, `propagate` (BFS with visited-set cycle guard), `shouldTrigger` (edge/level).
- **Trigger modes**: 'edge' (fire once on false->true) for event-like conditions (collision, clicks, timers); 'level' (fire every frame while true) for state-like conditions (always, keyPressed, actionInput/actionHeld, getVariable, and/or). Explicit `data.triggerMode` overrides; UI dropdown in the properties panel for condition blocks.
- **Edge rules** (left-to-right, matching the puzzle visual): from a condition, all orthogonally adjacent actions (legacy) + rightward same-row scan; from an action (chaining), ONLY rightward scan (no vertical chaining — avoids unintended triggers); eventId links scope to peers. Conditions are never implicitly gated by adjacency (they're independent event sources); fan-in (OR) works naturally.
- **Integration**: editor preview (`processDeterministicConditions` in script.js) and export player (`runCondition` in player-runtime.js) both use HydraFlow identically. `tests/flow.test.js` (new): chaining, fan-out, fan-in, cycle guard, trigger modes.
- **Also in this commit**: P4 item 37 batch 4 (scene management: Push Scene, Pop Scene, Set Scene Background + scene picker control) — was deferred pending flow, now lands together. Full suite ALL green.
- Next: item 36 phase C (flow tracer), then item 37 resumes.

## 2026-11-04 — Cycle 18 (improve): P4 item 37 batch 3 — audio control COMPLETED

- Implemented the third batch of the block-catalog expansion workstream: **audio control**. Five new blocks (block-catalog.js, auto-added to the palette): four **Audio** actions — **Pause Music** (pauses the streaming `<audio>` track keeping its position; a tone played on the Music bus cannot pause, documented in the note), **Resume Music** (continues a paused track; no-op when nothing is paused), **Stop All Sounds** (silences the music track plus every live SFX/UI voice via a new per-runtime stopper registry, then frees all bus voices at once), **Set Master Volume** (0-1 runtime fader); and one **Audio** condition — **Music Playing** (true while the music-bus voice is held and the track is not paused; invertible, so "the song ended" is Invert On).

The Audio tab gained a Master fader row above the three bus rows.
- Identical semantics in the editor preview and exported games: script.js got stopper registration in playToneBuffer/playSfxBuffer (osc.stop / src.stop / element pause), pausePreviewMusic/resumePreviewMusic/stopAllPreviewSounds, master-volume init per preview run + Set Master Volume executor case, `musicPaused` reset on new music plays, and `evaluateMusicPlayingCondition`; player-runtime.js got the mirror (state.masterVolume/musicPaused/liveSoundStoppers, pauseExportMusic/resumeExportMusic/stopAllExportSounds, 4 action cases + actionables list, `musicPlaying` condition + eval). Music Playing is audio-state only, not world-dependent, so it evaluates normally while paused in both runtimes.
- One test-authoring fix (mine, not an engine bug): normalizeMasterVolume(null) initially returned 0 because Number(null) is 0 — aligned the module and hydra-universal so null/undefined/empty mean "missing" (full volume).

## 2026-11-04 — Cycle 17 (improve): P4 item 37 batch 2 — text manipulation COMPLETED

- Implemented the second batch of the block-catalog expansion workstream: **text manipulation**. Six new blocks (block-catalog.js, auto-added to the palette): five **Object** actions — **Set Text Size** (font size 6–200, clamped; bad expressions evaluate to 0 and clamp to 6), **Set Text Color** (color picker), **Set Text Style** (Bold/Italic on/off writing the `bold`/`italic` flags both draw paths read), **Set Text Alignment** (left/center/right; corrupt values fall back to left), **Append Text** (extends `_runtimeText`, or the object's `text` when no runtime override exists — the typewriter building block); and one **Logic** condition — **Text Compare** (equals / not-equals / contains / startsWith / endsWith / isEmpty, invertible; corrupt operators behave as equals; non-text objects never match). All five actions and the condition touch only `kind === 'text'` objects, mirroring the existing setText guard. The **Position Check** condition gained a **Text length** property (a derived property computed from the displayed text in both evaluators, since `textLength` is not a stored field).
- Identical semantics in the editor preview and exported games: script.js got the five executor cases + `evaluateTextCheckCondition` + the `textLength` branch in `evaluatePositionCondition`; player-runtime.js got the five actions added to its actionables list, `textCheck` added to its conditions list, the five executor cases, `evalTextCheck`, and the `textLength` branch in `evalProperty`. The condition is text-read-only, not world-dependent, so it evaluates normally while paused in both runtimes.
- Two test-authoring fixes (mine, not engine bugs): Phase V assertions that place a setVariable action adjacent to a firing condition cell get triggered by adjacency — the engine fires actions next to a triggered cell — so the sprite-target and non-empty-isEmpty checks moved into their own sessions.
- Tests: `tests/export-boot.test.js` Phase V (size/color/style/alignment writes, non-text objects untouched, size clamping incl. bad-expression, corrupt alignment fallback, style-off clearing, appendText on runtime override + on plain text, all six textCheck operators incl. corrupt-operator-as-equals and reversed, non-text never matches, isEmpty on empty/non-empty text, positionCheck textLength, zero diagnostics); 8 new `tests/browser-smoke.js` checks (palette has all 6, Set Text Size placement + Size field, Set Text Color placement + color picker, Text Compare placement + Compare/Compare-to fields, Position Check offers Text length). Full suite ALL green (first run had 4 flagship-section flakes — `window.scenes` undefined in the hub2 page and a download timeout — unrelated to this change; clean re-run was all green).
- Docs: user-guide Objects section gained the text-manipulation paragraph; block-reference gained the 6 entries (Object 8 → 13, Logic 10 → 11) + updated Position Check properties (kept honest by `tests/docs.test.js`); ROADMAP item 37 checklist text-manipulation checked off; CHANGELOG entry added.
- Next cycle: item 37 batch 3 — audio control, per the checklist order.

## 2026-11-04 — Cycle 16 (improve): P4 item 37 batch 1 — sprite transforms COMPLETED

- Implemented the first batch of the block-catalog expansion workstream: **sprite transforms**. Two new **Object** blocks (block-catalog.js, auto-added to the palette): **Flip Object** (direction select: Flip horizontal / Flip vertical / Flip both / Unflip, writing flipH/flipV) and **Set Scale** (Scale X / Scale Y expression fields, writing scaleX/scaleY factors). The **Position Check** condition gained Scale X / Scale Y property options (evaluators read `obj[property]` directly, so both runtimes needed no condition changes).
- Semantics (identical in editor preview and exported games): flip + scale are **drawing-only** — applied about the object center in every draw path (sprites/shapes, text incl. button labels + backgrounds, panels), while collision keeps using width/height. The block notes say this plainly, pointing at Set Size for hitbox changes. `hydra-universal.js` normalizeObject defaults old projects to scale 1 / no flip and repairs corrupt scale values to 1; the draw paths coerce defensively (`Number.isFinite ? : 1`, `flipH ? -1 : 1`), so exported games (which never inline hydra-universal.js) and legacy objects draw fine. Edit-mode scene elements compose rotation + flip/scale in the CSS transform.
- Tests: `tests/export-boot.test.js` Phase U (flipObject horizontal/vertical/both/off, corrupt direction no-op, setScale factors, positionCheck reading scaleX, legacy objects without the fields drawing with zero warnings, zero diagnostics); 6 new `tests/browser-smoke.js` checks (palette, placement, Direction + Scale X/Y panel fields, Position Check property options, no page/console errors). Two test-authoring fixes: the flip assertions needed `!== true` instead of `=== false` because the exported runtime never writes the untouched flag (undefined behaves as off by coercion — correct engine behavior).
- Docs: user-guide Objects section gained the flip/scale paragraph; block-reference gained the 2 entries + updated Position Check properties (kept honest by `tests/docs.test.js`); ROADMAP item 37 sprite-transforms checked off; CHANGELOG entry added. Full suite ALL green.

## 2026-11-04 — Cycle 14 (improve): P4 item E3 mobile wrapper export COMPLETED

- Implemented the full E3 pillar (Capacitor project generation for Android + iOS). New shared pure module `hydra/hydra-mobileexport.js` (HydraMobileExport, `factorySource` per the hydra-*.js pattern, editor-side only — never inlined into games): `sanitizePackageName`/`sanitizeMobileAppId` (npm-safe name; Android-valid application IDs — each dot-segment starts with a letter, letters/digits/underscores only, dashes dropped because Android forbids them, leading digits stripped, e.g. "2 Fast 2 Furious" → `com.hydrascript.fast2furious`), `validMobileAppId`, `buildPackageJson` (complete buildable package.json: Capacitor @core/@cli/@android/@ios lockstep deps, @capacitor/assets devDep, honest scripts `sync`/`open:android`/`open:ios`/`build:apk` via gradlew assembleDebug), `buildCapacitorConfig` (plain-JSON capacitor.config.json — appId, appName, webDir "www", colors, iOS contentInset; no TypeScript toolchain to read), `buildReadme` (guided checklist: what the engine generated, the exact commands `npx cap add android` / `npx cap sync` / `gradlew assembleDebug`, copy-paste AndroidManifest prefs for orientation lock + fullscreen theme, the `npx capacitor-assets generate` icon/splash pipeline, the iOS flow, and the honest limits), `buildMobileFiles` (assembles package.json + capacitor.config.json + README.md + www/index.html + www/assets/* + resources/icon.png + resources/splash.png when PNG bytes are supplied; every entry a fresh Uint8Array; never throws).

- Tests: new `tests/mobileexport.test.js` (name/id sanitizing incl. dash + leading-digit rules, package.json/capacitor.config.json/README builders incl. hostile input, project assembly incl. byte-identical icon/splash, ZIP round-trip through HydraWebExport.buildZip); `tests/export-boot.test.js` Phase T (exposed on HydraPlayer, missing-module guard, classic export unaffected, complete project contents, package.json/capacitor.config.json/README contents, www/ payload boots verbatim with zero diagnostics, no data URLs, no http refs, icon/splash byte-identical, no-resources omission); `tests/browser-smoke.js` updated (5 target cards, mobile ZIP download with ZIP magic, real-archive check, larger than the desktop ZIP). ## 2026-11-04 — Cycle 12 (improve): P4 item E1 web targets COMPLETED

- Implemented the full E1 pillar (itch.io-ready ZIP + PWA export). New shared pure module `hydra/hydra-webexport.js` (HydraWebExport, `factorySource` for export-time use, same pattern as the other hydra-*.js modules): `sanitizeFilename`/`mimeExtension`/`parseDataUrl` (pure base64 decoder, no atob/DOM), `extractAssets` (deep-clones the project; every asset with a decodable dataUrl becomes a file under `assets/` with deduped names, records rewritten to `{name, kind, file}`, input never mutated, never throws), `buildZip` (dependency-free stored-method ZIP writer with CRC32, UTF-8 names), `buildManifest` (PWA manifest JSON), `buildServiceWorker` (offline-first: precache on install, cache-first GET fetch, stale-cache purge on activate), `injectPwa` (manifest link + theme-color into `<head>`, SW registration before `</body>`), `drawIcon` (navy/amber app icon from the game's initial on any 2D context), `pwaCacheName`.
- `HydraPlayer.buildWebTarget(project, {pwa})` in player-runtime.js: extracts assets, builds the playable HTML against the file-based project, optionally adds manifest + SW + PWA injection. The HydraWebExport load guard is LAZY (throws a clear error only when buildWebTarget is called), so the classic single-file export never needs the module. Exported runtime resolves external assets through the new `HydraAudio.assetUrl` (dataUrl wins, else relative `assets/<file>`); `resolveSoundSource` now recognizes `{file}` records with its return shape unchanged (sound.test.js untouched and green); `image()` in player-runtime uses it too, so sprites, backgrounds, and tileset images all load relatively. SFX over relative paths streams through the existing `<audio>` fallback (`decodeDataUrl` returns null for non-data URLs — no new code path).
- Editor UI: the Export button now opens a target-picker dialog (index.html markup + style.css): Single HTML / Website ZIP (itch.io) / Installable app (PWA) cards, each with a guided checklist (what the engine generated, what the user does next, what cannot be automated and why). project.js wires the three downloads with busy states: standalone keeps the old one-file flow; Web ZIP zips index.html + assets/; PWA adds manifest + sw.js + canvas-rendered 192/512 PNG icons. Escape/backdrop/X close the dialog.
- Four real bugs the tests caught: (1) `buildZip` used `instanceof Uint8Array`, which fails for bytes created in another realm (vm sandbox in tests) — now duck-typed via `toBytes`, a genuine robustness fix. (2) `injectPwa` replaced the FIRST `</body>`, but the runtime source embeds template literals containing `</body>` (retro-FPS template) — the SW registration landed mid-script and corrupted the export; now targets the LAST `</head>`/`</body>`. (3) Test-only ZIP reader used the local-header CRC offset for the central directory (spec: CRC at 16 there, not 14). (4) Test expectations: cross-realm `instanceof` again, and `extractAssets` correctly returns 3 files when 3 assets have data URLs.
- Tests: new `tests/webexport.test.js` (filename/mime/data-URL helpers, extraction incl. dedupe + no-input-mutation + dangling assets, ZIP round-trip through a minimal reader with CRC verification, manifest/SW/injection/icon painters, hostile-input safety); `tests/export-boot.test.js` Phase R (missing-module guard, classic export unaffected without the module, asset bytes byte-identical in the file set, no embedded data URLs in the web HTML, PWA manifest/SW/index injection, verbatim boot of the PWA index.html with sprite image src === `assets/hero.png` and SFX Audio src === `assets/jump.mp3`, zero diagnostics); `tests/browser-smoke.js` updated (dialog opens with 3 cards + checklists, Escape/X close, standalone download flow through the dialog unchanged, Web ZIP + PWA ZIP downloads carry real ZIP magic, PWA ZIP larger). Full suite ALL green.
- Docs: user-guide "Export a playable game" section now covers the three targets; ROADMAP E1 checked off; CHANGELOG entry added.
- Next cycle: E2 — Desktop wrapper export (Electron + electron-builder project generation), per the roadmap's priority chain (E1 → E2 → E3 → E4 → block coverage → 3D pillar → 34 → 35 → P2).

## 2026-11-04 — Cycle 11 (improve): P4 item 32 physics upgrade COMPLETED

- Implemented the full physics-upgrade pillar. New shared pure module `hydra/hydra-physics2d.js` (HydraPhysics2D, `factorySource` for export inlining, same pattern as hydra-tiles.js): `normalizePhysics2d` (collision fields repair to box/solid/not-one-way/layer-0/mask-255 — old projects behave exactly as before; never throws; also taught `num()` that null/undefined means "missing", not 0), `hitboxOf`/`circleOf` (circle centered on hitbox, radius = min side / 2), `canCollide` (layer/mask gating: each mask must include the other's layer), `pairOverlap` (box/box, circle/circle, box/circle with the normal pointing from B to A; symmetric tolerance), `resolvePair` (cross-from-side resolution for box pairs — the engine's long-standing semantics preserved exactly; MTV push for pairs with a circle; one-way platforms resolve ONLY from above while falling, everything else passes through), `triggerContacts` (pure helper returning {zone, obj} pairs, layer-gated), `raycast` (segment vs box/circle, closest hit with point/distance/object, layer mask), `overlapCircle` (circle probe query, layer mask).
- Both runtimes route through the module. Editor (script.js): `resolveCollisions` rewritten (triggers report contacts instead of blocking; per-frame `_triggerContacts`/`_triggerPairs`/`_triggerEntered`/`_triggerExited` edge state, reset on preview launch and scene switch), `resolveTilemapCollisions` layer-gated, new Inspector **Collision** section (Shape select, Collider Solid/Trigger select, One-way platform checkbox, Layer 0-7 select, Collides-with 8-layer checkboxes; skipped for tilemaps which collide per-tile), Collision/Overlap conditions shape-aware with exact legacy box semantics kept (tolerance split tol/2 per shape to match the old expand-one-box math; box/box pairs keep legacy side info), On Floor + grounded checks layer-gated. Export (player-runtime.js): same semantics, HydraPhysics2D load guard + `factorySource` inlined by `buildPlayableHtml`, trigger edge state in createState/loadScene, dead `overlap()` helper removed.
- Five new **Collision** blocks (block-catalog.js, auto-added to the palette): **Trigger Enter** / **Trigger Exit** (conditions, edge-triggered — fire once per touch start/end), **Raycast Hit** (condition: From X/Y, To X/Y, layer mask; reports the closest hit), **Objects Overlapping** (condition: circle center + radius + layer mask), **Set Collision Layer** (action: runtime layer/mask change). All four conditions are world-dependent, so they report the freeze while paused like Collision/On Floor (mirrors the exported runtime).
- Three real bugs the tests caught: (1) `loadScene` zeroes velX/velY, so test objects with initial velocities never moved — tests now set velocity through the Set Velocity block (a test-authoring lesson, not an engine bug). (2) The upward-raycast test ray grazed the falling hero's left edge — the raycast was correct, the test geometry was wrong (hero moved clear). (3) The physics2d test used degenerate expectations for a circle centered exactly on a box corner plus missing object ids — fixed the test; also fixed `num(null)` returning 0 instead of the default. Also caught mid-implementation: my block-catalog edit initially dropped the Raycast Hit `toY` field (fixed before any test ran), and an export-boot edit landed Phase Q mid-file (moved to the end, removing a duplicated exit line).
- Tests: new `tests/physics2d.test.js` (48 checks: normalization, shapes, overlaps, layers/masks, resolution incl. one-way from-below pass-through, trigger contacts, raycast, overlap queries, hostile-input safety); `tests/export-boot.test.js` Phase Q (export inlining, circle body landing on a box floor, trigger enter firing exactly once + pass-through, trigger exit edge, one-way jump-through + land-from-above, layer exclusion vs default collision, raycast hit/miss, overlap probe hit/miss, runtime Set Collision Layer, corrupt collision fields, zero diagnostics); 9 new `tests/browser-smoke.js` checks (module loaded, Collision section, Shape/Collider/One-way/Layer/Collides-with fields, shape select writes circle to the object, 5 palette blocks, Trigger Enter + Raycast Hit placement and property panels). Full suite ALL green.
- Docs: new "Physics upgrade" user-guide section + Collision category grown to 7 entries in the block reference (kept honest by `tests/docs.test.js`); ROADMAP item 32 checked off; CHANGELOG entry added.
- Next cycle: E1 — Web targets (itch.io-ready ZIP export + PWA export), the first item in the roadmap's new priority chain (32 → E1-E4 → block coverage → 3D pillar → 34 → 35 → P2).

## 2026-11-04 — Cycle 10 (improve): P4 item 31 tilemaps COMPLETED

## 2026-11-04 — Cycle 9 (improve): P4 item 30 audio buses COMPLETED

- Implemented the full audio-bus pillar. New shared pure module `hydra/hydra-buses.js` (HydraAudioBuses, `factorySource` for export inlining, same pattern as hydra-actions.js): fixed Music/SFX/UI buses with `BUS_LABELS`/`BUS_DEFAULTS` (music 0.8 vol / 1 voice, sfx 1.0 / 8 voices, ui 1.0 / 4 voices), `normalizeBuses` (corrupt data repairs to defaults, unknown ids dropped, volumes clamped 0-1, maxVoices floored at 1; never throws), `normalizeBusId` (unknown/blank → 'sfx', so old Play Sound blocks behave exactly as before), `normalizePitch` (0.25–4), `busVolume`/`effectiveVolume` (muted → 0; final gain = bus × block volume), `createVoices`/`tryAcquire`/`release` (polyphony accounting; over-limit sounds dropped), and a pure base64 `decodeDataUrl` (no atob/DOM needed) so the runtimes hand ArrayBuffers to `decodeAudioData` without a fetch round trip.
- New **Audio** inspector tab (fa-volume-up) in the editor: per-bus volume sliders (0–100) + mute toggles with a plain-language note per bus (music streams, one track at a time, loops by default; sfx 8 voices memory-resident; ui 4 voices memory-resident). Buses stored on `project.audioBuses` (normalizeProject defaults old projects to the shipped mix).
- **Play Sound** block gained Bus (Music/SFX/UI), Pitch (0.25–2), and a music-only Loop switch (music loops by default; old blocks store no loop → music-default true, sfx unaffected). Three new **Audio** blocks (block-catalog.js, auto-added to the palette): **Set Bus Volume**, **Set Bus Muted**, **Stop Music**.
- Playback semantics (identical in editor preview and exported games): music-bus assets stream through an `<audio>` element (never decoded into memory; `loop`/`playbackRate` from the block; a new track replaces the running one); SFX/UI assets decode once into an AudioBuffer kept memory-resident (buffer source per play, `playbackRate` = pitch); tones go through an oscillator with frequency × pitch; muted/zero-volume buses play nothing and take no voice. Fresh bus mix + voices per preview run and per export session; runtime blocks mutate the running mix only.
- Two test-authoring lessons: (1) `instanceof Uint8Array` fails across the vm sandbox boundary (sandbox has its own constructor) — duck-type the bytes check, same as the item-29 Set lesson. (2) The engine fires actions adjacent to the condition cell before row-scanning the rest (cell 60 ran before cell 1), so multi-action assertions must match by value, not by creation order.
- Tests: new `tests/audio-buses.test.js` (normalization, coercion, volume math, voice accounting, decoder, corrupt-data safety); `tests/export-boot.test.js` Phase O (export inlining, music streaming + loop + bus×sound volume, SFX decoded exactly once across 9 plays, 8-voice cap dropping the 9th, pitch doubling, music replace, setBusVolume/setBusMuted/stopMusic, corrupt audioBuses, zero diagnostics); Phase G updated to the new behavior (SFX-bus assets decode to memory-resident buffers, music-bus assets stream); 12 new `tests/browser-smoke.js` checks (Audio tab, 3 buses, sliders/mutes, mute toggle, palette blocks, Set Bus Volume placement + panel, Play Sound Bus/Pitch fields, Music-bus Loop field, module loaded). Full suite ALL green.
- Docs: new "Audio buses" user-guide section + 3 block-reference entries under Catalog blocks → Audio (3) + updated Play Sound entry (kept honest by `tests/docs.test.js`); ROADMAP item 30 checked off; CHANGELOG entry added.

## 2026-11-04 — Cycle 8 (improve): P4 item 29 action-based input COMPLETED

- Implemented the full input-actions pillar. New shared pure module `hydra/hydra-actions.js` (HydraActions, `factorySource` for export inlining, same pattern as hydra-particles.js): `normalizeActions` (repairs corrupt project data — blank/duplicate names dropped, bad gamepad/touch coerced, legacy single `key` field still works; never throws), `createRuntimeState` (private defaults copy so `resetBindings` always restores the shipped bindings), per-frame `update` from keyboard key sets + pressed gamepad buttons + active touch zones with edge detection for gamepad/touch sources, `remapAction` (runtime key rebinding, returns false on unknown action/blank key), `resetBindings`, `pollGamepadButtons` (injected getter so the module stays DOM-free), `zonesAt` (a point sits in one horizontal + one vertical zone, so multi-touch drives two actions at once), and a one-tap `defaultPlatformerActions` preset.
- New **Inputs** inspector tab (fa-gamepad) in the editor: add/rename/delete actions, click-to-capture key binding (primary + alt key; Escape cancels), gamepad button select (16 standard buttons), touch-zone select (none/left/right/top/bottom half). Wand button adds the platformer preset (Move Left, Move Right, Jump, Shoot, Interact) with sensible keys/gamepad/touch bindings. Actions stored on `project.inputActions` (normalizeProject defaults old projects to `[]` — old projects behave exactly as before).
- Three new **Input** blocks (block-catalog.js, auto-added to the palette by `hydrateCatalogBlocks`): **Action Input** (condition, Held/Just pressed/Released via inputCondition, invertible, Action field is a new `action` control — a select populated from the Inputs tab), **Remap Action** (action + new key; the settings-menu building block), **Reset Action Bindings** (restores Inputs-tab defaults). Editor (script.js): `evaluateActionCondition` mirrors the export; `remapAction`/`resetActionBindings` executor cases; per-frame `HydraActions.update` before scripts run (runs while paused, like raw key input); touch listeners on the preview canvas route touches through `zonesAt`; gamepad polled via `navigator.getGamepads`. Export (player-runtime.js): HydraActions load guard + `factorySource` inlined by `buildPlayableHtml`; `state.actions` + `state.touchZones`; per-frame update before processScope; `evalActionInput`/`remapAction`/`resetActionBindings` cases; all export-boot/fps-export/flagship phases now load hydra-actions.js (the guard throws otherwise).
- Two real bugs the tests caught: (1) `initInputActionsPanel()` ran at script load when `window.projectManager` is still undefined (project.js assigns it on DOMContentLoaded) — a bare `projectManager` reference threw a ReferenceError and halted the rest of script.js, so no create-button handlers attached and panel creation silently did nothing (smoke test caught it). Fixed by using `window.projectManager?.getCurrentProject?.()` in the input-actions helpers. (2) `update()` used `instanceof Set` on the source sets, which fails across vm sandbox boundaries (different Set constructors) — switched to duck-typing via `asSet`.
- Tests: new `tests/actions.test.js` (normalization, keyboard held/just-pressed/released + alias matching, gamepad routing + poll safety, touch zones + multi-touch, remap/reset, corrupt-data safety, platformer preset); `tests/export-boot.test.js` Phase N (export inlining, key-driven just-pressed/held/released through the real loop, touch zone, gamepad RB via stubbed navigator, remap, reset, corrupt inputActions, zero diagnostics); 9 new `tests/browser-smoke.js` checks (Inputs tab, add/rename, key capture, platformer preset, palette blocks, Action Input placement, Action/When panel fields, action select lists defined actions, module loaded). Full suite ALL green.
- Docs: new "Action-based input" user-guide section + 3 block-reference entries under Catalog blocks → Input (4) (kept honest by `tests/docs.test.js`); ROADMAP item 29 checked off; CHANGELOG entry added.

## 2026-11-04 — Cycle 7 (improve): P4 item 28 CPU particle system COMPLETED

- Implemented the full particle pillar. New shared pure module `hydra/hydra-particles.js` (HydraParticles, `factorySource` for export inlining, same pattern as hydra-tween.js): one-shot bursts and named continuous emitters with rate + optional follow-object (pins the emitter to a named object's center each frame); velocity + spread around a direction (0 = right, 90 = down, 270 = up); gravity; color/size interpolation over lifetime with alpha fade; slight per-particle speed/lifetime randomization so bursts look natural; additive blending via a two-pass draw (normal, then 'lighter'); pooled cap of 1500 (oldest recycled first, counters tracked); corrupt-spec normalization that never throws; pause handled by the runtimes (they skip update while paused, so particles freeze mid-air and resume exactly).
- Four new Particles blocks (block-catalog.js, auto-added to the palette by `hydrateCatalogBlocks`): **Emit Burst** (5 presets: Explosion, Sparkle, Smoke puff, Confetti, Splash), **Start Emitter** (5 presets: Fire, Magic trail, Rain, Snow, Fountain; named, replace-on-restart), **Stop Emitter** (blank name = all; in-flight particles finish out), **Clear Particles** (blank name = everything). Preset-driven property panels in the editor (hand-editing any field flips the preset to Custom, same pattern as the tween panel); X/Y are expression inputs (`hero.posX, hero.posY`); Stop/Clear have plain Emitter-name panels.
- Editor (script.js): previewState.particles created fresh per preview run and per scene load; per-frame `HydraParticles.update` with a follow resolver before scripts run (inside the pause gate); `HydraParticles.draw` between the world pass and the HUD pass (world-space, camera-applied, offscreen-culled); action cases with expression-evaluated x/y. Export (player-runtime.js): HydraParticles load guard + `factorySource` inlined by `buildPlayableHtml`; same init/update/draw/action semantics (identical follow-resolver math); all export-boot/fps-export/flagship phases now load hydra-particles.js (the guard throws otherwise).
- Tests: new `tests/particles.test.js` (60 checks: module surface, spec normalization, burst spawn/origin/colors, gravity/motion/lifetime, emitter start/replace/stop-all, fractional emission accumulation, follow-object pinning + throwing-followFn safety, pool cap + recycled counter, clearParticles scoping, draw arcs/culling/additive composite-op switching, null-safety); `tests/export-boot.test.js` Phase M (export inlining, gameStart burst at an expression position, ~rate spawning over a second, follow-object pinning to the hero center, pause freezing positions + lifetimes, resume, stopEmitter keeping in-flight particles, clearParticles, zero diagnostics); 7 new `tests/browser-smoke.js` checks (Particles category, 4 palette blocks, Emit Burst + Start Emitter placement, Preset/Count/Speed/Lifetime panel fields, Emitter name + Follow object fields, module loaded in the editor page). Full suite ALL green.
- Docs: new "Particles" user-guide section + 4 block-reference entries under Catalog blocks → Particles (4) (kept honest by `tests/docs.test.js`); ROADMAP item 28 checked off; CHANGELOG entry added.

## 2026-11-04 — Cycle 6 (improve): P4 flagship item F5 Hydra Quest template COMPLETED

- Implemented the flagship game template, closing out Ky'lin's F1-F5 directive. **Hydra Quest** (`flagship` template id, featured first in the New Project gallery with a Featured badge) is a complete multi-level platformer built ENTIRELY with engine blocks — 405 blocks across 8 scenes, zero hand-written game code: Main Menu (Play, How to Play, best score, Reset progress), How to Play, Level Select (levels 2-3 locked until earned), 3 platforming levels (Green Hills, Sky Bridges with a pit, Ember Caverns) each with coins, patrolling enemies, an anchored HUD (score/lives), a pause menu (P key or the II button), a per-level goal, and a 3-layer timer music loop, plus You Win (best score) and Game Over (retries the level you died in) screens. Level-unlock progression persists via the "HydraQuest" save slot, shared between preview and exports. Dogfoods every pillar: F2 UI widgets (button/panel/anchor/HUD pause menus and HUD), F3 save slots (unlocks + best score), F4 pause (pause menu is plain blocks), sprite animations, named timers, screen shake, camera follow, tone-synth SFX/music.

Fixed at the engine level, not the test: new `HydraSave.safeStorage()` returns null when storage is unreadable, and both runtimes (player-runtime.js saveGame/loadGame/deleteSaveSlot/saveSlotExists, script.js preview equivalents) now route through it — saves degrade to safe no-ops and the game keeps playing. Covered by new `tests/save-slots.test.js` checks (throwing-scope safeStorage, null-storage no-ops).
- Tests: new `tests/flagship.test.js` (54 checks: template registration + featured flag, 8 scenes/ids/names, every block type known to the runtime, every object/animation/scene/slot reference resolves, PNG validity, normalizeProject round trip with assets + animations intact, real export boot, then a full scripted playthrough in the exported runtime: menu → level select with lock gating → Level 1 (pause/resume menu, coin collect, enemy hit + respawn) → goal unlock + save → Level 2 → Level 3 → win with best score → play again → game over → retry, ending with ZERO runtime diagnostics and zero console warnings); 13 new `tests/browser-smoke.js` checks (featured card + badge, 8 scenes, 9 assets, flagship preview with no error overlay, flagship export download + standalone boot with no page errors). ## 2026-11-04 — Cycle 5 (improve): P4 flagship item F4 pause/resume COMPLETED

- Implemented the full engine-pause pillar. New shared pure module `hydra/hydra-pause.js` (HydraPause, `factorySource` for export inlining, same pattern as hydra-save.js): `pause`/`resume`/`toggle`/`isPaused` with legacy-state coercion (pre-pause states read as unpaused), plus a frozen game clock — the runtime reports each paused frame's real length via `notePausedFrame`, and `gameNow` subtracts accumulated paused time so named timers hold their elapsed time and resume exactly where they left off.
- Pause semantics (identical in editor preview and exported games): while paused the world simulation freezes — physics integration, collision resolution, sprite animations, tweens, camera follow/shake, named timers — but the game keeps rendering every frame, button hover/press states keep updating, and script grids keep running, so a pause menu is plain blocks: Key Just Pressed (P) → Pause Game; Game Paused → Show pause panel; Object Clicked (Resume) → Resume Game + Hide menu. World-dependent conditions report the freeze instead of stale contacts: Collision, Overlap/Touch, On Floor, On Screen Edge do not fire while paused, and On Timer never ticks on a paused frame. Scene switches and preview restarts always unpause.
- Four new Game Flow blocks (block-catalog.js, auto-added to the palette by `hydrateCatalogBlocks`): **Pause Game**, **Resume Game**, **Toggle Pause** (one-block pause key), and the invertible **Game Paused** condition. Editor (script.js): previewState pause fields, launchPreview/loadPreviewScene reset, gameLoop gating (world sim skipped while paused; UI widget states + scripts + draw always run), timer frozen-clock + paused gate, condition gating, action cases. Export (player-runtime.js): HydraPause load guard + `factorySource` inlined by `buildPlayableHtml`; createState/loadScene fields; loop gating; `evalTimer` frozen clock + gate; same condition gating and action/condition cases; all export-boot/fps-export phases now load hydra-pause.js (the guard throws otherwise).
- Tests: `tests/pause.test.js` (pure module: transitions, idempotency, legacy/null-state safety, clock freeze across pause/resume cycles, bad-input never throws); `tests/export-boot.test.js` Phase L (export inlining, physics frozen while paused, Game Paused firing + reversed not firing, scripts still running, collision gated on permanently-overlapping static pair then firing after resume, timer frozen ~100ms then resuming, resume/toggle round-trips through the inlined module, zero diagnostics); new `tests/browser-smoke.js` checks (Game Flow category, 4 palette blocks, Pause Game + Game Paused placement, Invert field). Real bug the smoke test caught: after placing a block, selecting the cell swaps the side panel to the property panel (`showPropertiesPanel` hides `#block-type-panel`) — the new checks click `#back-to-blocks-btn` first; this is intended UI behavior, not a regression.
- Docs: new "Pause and resume" user-guide section with the canonical pause-menu wiring + 4 block-reference entries (kept honest by `tests/docs.test.js`); ROADMAP F4 checked off; CHANGELOG entries added.
- Full suite ALL green (15 syntax + 15 node files + Playwright smoke). Committed as one F4 commit.
- Next cycle: F5 — the flagship multi-level platformer template (main menu, 3 levels, HUD, pause menu, win/game-over, level unlocks via save slots, sprite animations, sound) built ENTIRELY with engine blocks, completable in preview AND as an exported standalone.
